```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }



    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale!=0f)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected paused surgery required.");
        const string key="wim023-observe-surgery:1";
        if(SessionState.GetBool(key,false))throw new InvalidOperationException("Observer already registered; inspect existing run.");
        var type=T("ISurgeryQuery");object query=R(type);var get=type.GetMethod("TryGetOrder");
        object[] initial={"surgery:1",null};
        if(!(bool)get.Invoke(query,initial)||(string)P(initial[1],"selectedPartInstanceId")!="surgical-part:1"
          ||Convert.ToString(P(initial[1],"state"))=="Completed")throw new InvalidOperationException("Expected first order unavailable.");
        var partType=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");
        var part=((IEnumerable)partType.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(R(partType),null)).Cast<object>()
            .Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:1");
        string selectedStack=(string)P(part,"worldStackId"),selectedInstance=(string)P(part,"physicalItemInstanceId");
        if(!SessionState.GetBool("wim023-ready-needs:"+selectedInstance+":surgery:1",false))
          throw new InvalidOperationException("Matching once-per-order need preparation missing.");
        var itemType=T("IWorldItemStackRuntime");object items=R(itemType);bool sawRoutable=false;
        
        var doctor=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>()
          .Single(a=>a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&a.gameObject.activeInHierarchy&&(string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")=="owner");
        var patient=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>()
          .Single(a=>a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&a.gameObject.activeInHierarchy&&(string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")=="character:staff:1:01");
        var table=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("BuildableObject")).OfType<Component>()
          .Single(f=>f.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&Convert.ToString(P(f,"PersistentInstanceId"))==(string)P(initial[1],"facilityId"));
        var workAbility=doctor.GetType().GetMethods().Single(m=>m.Name=="GetAbility"&&m.IsGenericMethodDefinition)
          .MakeGenericMethod(T("AbilityWork")).Invoke(doctor,null);
        var assign=workAbility.GetType().GetMethods().Single(m=>m.Name=="TrySetPriorityWorkTarget"&&m.GetParameters().Length==4&&m.GetParameters()[1].ParameterType.Name=="WorkTypeId");
        object surgeryId=Activator.CreateInstance(assign.GetParameters()[1].ParameterType,new object[]{"work:surgery"});
        bool preparedJob=false;

        double start=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;float gameStart=Time.time;int last=-1;
        SessionState.SetBool(key,true);
        EditorApplication.CallbackFunction tick=null;
        tick=()=>{
          try{
            if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying)throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected Play ended during observation.");
            object[] args={"surgery:1",null};
            if(!(bool)get.Invoke(query,args))throw new InvalidOperationException("Observed order disappeared.");
            object order=args[1];
            if((bool)P(patient,"IsDead"))throw new InvalidOperationException("PREPARATION_PATIENT_DIED");

            if(!preparedJob && !(bool)P(order,"resultRolled")){
              object[] candidate={table,null};object[] eligibility={order,doctor,null};
              if((bool)type.GetMethod("TryGetWorkFor").Invoke(query,candidate)
                &&(string)P(candidate[1],"orderId")==(string)P(order,"orderId")
                &&(bool)type.GetMethod("CanOperate").Invoke(query,eligibility)){
                if(!SessionState.GetBool("wim023-cancel-fixture-clinical:"+selectedInstance,false)){
                  var persistenceType=T("ISurgeryPersistence");var persistence=R(persistenceType);
                  string beforeMedical=JsonUtility.ToJson(persistenceType.GetMethod("Capture").Invoke(persistence,null));
                  foreach(var actor in new[]{doctor,patient}){
                    var stats=P(actor,"Stats");var change=actor.GetType().GetMethod("ChangesStat");
                    var ct=change.GetParameters()[0].ParameterType;
                    var read=stats.GetType().GetMethod("GetConditionValue",new[]{ct,typeof(float)});
                    foreach(string n in new[]{"HUNGER","THIRST","SLEEP","FUN","EXCRETION","HYGIENE"}){
                      object c=Enum.Parse(ct,n);float before=(float)read.Invoke(stats,new object[]{c,0f});
                      change.Invoke(actor,new object[]{c,100f-before});
                    }
                  }
                  if(beforeMedical!=JsonUtility.ToJson(persistenceType.GetMethod("Capture").Invoke(persistence,null)))
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("Fixture need preparation changed medical authority");
                  SessionState.SetBool("wim023-cancel-fixture-clinical:"+selectedInstance,true);
                }
                var wt=T("ISurgeryWorkCommand");var work=R(wt);object[] reserve={table,doctor,null,null};
                if(!(bool)wt.GetMethod("TryReserveWork").Invoke(work,reserve))
                  throw new InvalidOperationException("Fixture work reservation rejected:"+reserve[3]);
                object[] apply={(string)P(order,"orderId"),doctor,(float)P(order,"requiredWork"),false,null};
                bool success=(bool)wt.GetMethod("ApplyWork").Invoke(work,apply);
                Debug.Log("WIM023 CANCEL_FIXTURE_DIRECT_WORK setupOnly=true;success="+success+";completed="+apply[3]+";failure="+apply[4]);
                if(!success&&(bool)P(order,"resultRolled"))throw new InvalidOperationException("Fixture outcome not finalized");
                preparedJob=(bool)P(order,"resultRolled");
              }
            }

            int window=(int)((Time.time-gameStart)/30f);
            bool terminal=!(bool)P(order,"IsActive");
            bool expired=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start>=120d||Time.time-gameStart>=240f;
            if(window!=last||terminal||expired){
              last=window;
              if(!sawRoutable){
                var routed=((IEnumerable)itemType.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(items,null)).Cast<object>()
                    .SingleOrDefault(s=>(string)P(s,"StackId")==selectedStack);
                if(routed!=null&&(string)P(routed,"DestinationId")==(string)P(order,"materialDestinationId")){
                  if(Convert.ToString(P(routed,"State"))=="FacilityOutputBuffer"
                    ||(int)P(routed,"Quantity")!=1||(string)P(routed,"ItemInstanceId")!=selectedInstance)
                      throw new InvalidOperationException("Selected output retarget state/identity/quantity invalid.");
                  sawRoutable=true;
                  Debug.Log("WIM023 SELECTED_OUTPUT_ROUTABLE_PASS stack="+selectedStack+";instance="+selectedInstance+";state="+P(routed,"State")+";quantity=1;position="+P(routed,"Position"));
                }
              }
              Debug.Log("WIM023 CANCEL_BOOTSTRAP_WINDOW order=surgery:1;state="+P(order,"state")+";work="+P(order,"completedWork")+"/"+P(order,"requiredWork")
                +";doctor="+P(order,"doctorId")+";patientAdmitted="+P(order,"patientAdmitted")+";materialConsumed="+P(order,"materialsConsumed")
                +";admission="+P(order,"admissionX")+","+P(order,"admissionY")+";status="+P(P(order,"statusData"),"code")+";gameElapsed="+(Time.time-gameStart)+";realElapsed="+(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start));
            }
            if(terminal||expired){
              EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(key,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
              Debug.Log("WIM023 CANCEL_BOOTSTRAP_OBSERVER_TERMINAL cause="+(terminal?"ORDER_TERMINAL":"BOUNDED_OBSERVATION_END")+";state="+P(order,"state")+";result="+P(order,"resultSucceeded"));
            }
          }catch(Exception ex){
            EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(key,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
            Debug.LogError("WIM023 CANCEL_BOOTSTRAP_OBSERVER_ERROR "+ex);
          }
        };
        EditorApplication.update+=tick;Time.timeScale=8f;EditorApplication.isPaused=false;
        result.Log("WIM023 CANCEL_BOOTSTRAP_OBSERVER_STARTED order=surgery:1;maxReal=120;maxGame=240;scale=8;initialGame={0};initialReal={1};directWorkPreparationOnly=true",gameStart,start);
    }
}
```

#### Cancellation cases
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }




    static object[] Rows(object v)=>((IEnumerable)v).Cast<object>().ToArray();
    static void Require(bool ok,string why){if(!ok)throw new InvalidOperationException(why);}
    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result) {
      Require(EditorApplication.isPlaying&&EditorApplication.isPaused&&Time.timeScale==0f
        &&!SessionState.GetBool("wim023-observe-surgery:1",false),"Paused bootstrap terminal required");
      const string observerKey="wim023-cancel-projection-observer";
      Require(!SessionState.GetBool(observerKey,false),"Existing cancellation observer");
      var qt=T("ISurgeryQuery");var query=R(qt);object[] historical={"surgery:1",null};
      Require((bool)qt.GetMethod("TryGetOrder").Invoke(query,historical)
        &&Convert.ToString(P(historical[1],"state"))=="Completed"
        &&(bool)P(historical[1],"resultSucceeded"),"Installed first-part bootstrap missing");
      Require(Rows(qt.GetProperty("ActiveOrders").GetValue(query)).Length==0,"Unexpected active order");
      var pt=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");var parts=R(pt);
      var part=Rows(pt.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null)).Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:2");
      string physical=(string)P(part,"physicalItemInstanceId");
      Require(!(bool)P(part,"installed")&&string.IsNullOrEmpty((string)P(part,"reservedOrderId")),"Incoming part not free");
      var it=T("IWorldItemStackRuntime");var items=R(it);
      var patient=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>()
        .Single(a=>a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&a.gameObject.activeInHierarchy&&(string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")=="character:staff:1:01");
      Require(!(bool)P(patient,"IsDead"),"Living patient required");
      var ct=T("ISurgeryCommandService");var commands=R(ct);
      var at=T("IAnatomyHealthRuntime");var anatomy=R(at);
      var dt=T("ISurgeryMaterialDestinationRuntime");var destinations=R(dt);
      var admissionType=T("IFacilityBufferMassAdmissionService");var admission=R(admissionType);
      var table=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("BuildableObject")).OfType<Component>()
        .Single(f=>f.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&Convert.ToString(P(f,"PersistentInstanceId"))==(string)P(historical[1],"facilityId"));
      int caseIndex=0,phase=0;object order=null;string stackId=null,destination=null,fingerprint=null,token=null,settled=null,settledPart=null;
      double caseReal=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;float phaseGame=Time.time,caseGame=Time.time;
      Action schedule=()=>{
        Require(Rows(qt.GetProperty("ActiveOrders").GetValue(query)).Length==0,"Previous authority not closed");
        part=Rows(pt.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null)).Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:2");
        var stock=Rows(it.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(items,null)).Single(s=>(string)P(s,"ItemInstanceId")==physical);
        Require((int)P(stock,"Quantity")==1&&(int)P(stock,"ReservedQuantity")==0&&string.IsNullOrEmpty((string)P(part,"reservedOrderId")),"Selected stock not released");
        stackId=(string)P(stock,"StackId");
        object[] args={P(historical[1],"subject"),"procedure:prosthetic-installation","arm:left","surgical-part:2","owner",P(historical[1],"facilityId"),null,null};
        Require((bool)ct.GetMethod("TrySchedule").Invoke(commands,args),"Real schedule rejected:"+args[7]);
        order=args[6];destination=(string)P(order,"materialDestinationId");fingerprint=(string)P(order,"materialCapacityFingerprint");
        Require((string)P(order,"replacementExpectedOldPartId")=="surgical-part:1"&&(bool)P(order,"OwnsMaterialAuthority")
          &&!(bool)P(order,"resultRolled")&&(float)P(order,"completedWork")==0f,"Planned replacement not captured");
        token=null;
        if(caseIndex>0){
          var body=at.GetMethod("GetAnatomySnapshot",new[]{typeof(string)}).Invoke(anatomy,new object[]{"character:staff:1:01"});
          var node=Rows(P(body,"Nodes")).Single(n=>(string)P(n,"nodeId")=="arm:left");
          object[] reserve={order,node,P(table,"centerPos"),admission,null};
          Require((bool)T("ISurgicalPartReplacementRuntime").GetMethod("TryReserveReplacementOutput").Invoke(parts,reserve),
            "Explicit reserved-phase fixture rejected:"+reserve[4]);
          Require(Convert.ToString(P(order,"replacementPhase"))=="OutputReserved","Missing real output token");
          token=(string)P(order,"replacementAdmissionTokenId");
        }
        object[] validate={order,null};
        Require((bool)dt.GetMethod("TryValidate").Invoke(destinations,validate),"Initial live material authority invalid:"+validate[1]);
        Require((string)P(order,"materialCapacityFingerprint")==fingerprint,"Phase preparation changed frozen fingerprint");
        phase=0;phaseGame=caseGame=Time.time;caseReal=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;
        Debug.Log("WIM023 CANCEL_CASE_STARTED index="+caseIndex+";order="+P(order,"orderId")+";phase="+P(order,"replacementPhase")
          +";reservedPhaseSetupOnly="+(caseIndex>0)+";fingerprint="+fingerprint);
      };
      schedule();
      SessionState.SetBool(observerKey,true);
      EditorApplication.CallbackFunction tick=null;
      tick=()=>{
        try{
          Require(EditorApplication.isPlaying,"Temporary world ended");
          Require(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-caseReal<30d&&Time.time-caseGame<30f,"Cancellation did not settle within case bound");
          if(EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale<=0f||Time.time<=phaseGame)return;
          if(phase==0){
            Require((bool)P(order,"IsActive")&&!(bool)P(order,"resultRolled")&&(float)P(order,"completedWork")==0f
              &&!(bool)P(patient,"IsDead"),"Living waiting order changed before cancellation");
            if(caseIndex<2){
              object[] cancel={P(order,"orderId"),null};
              Require((bool)ct.GetMethod("TryCancel").Invoke(commands,cancel),"Public cancel rejected:"+cancel[1]);
            }else{
              patient.GetType().GetMethod("ApplyDamage",new[]{typeof(float),typeof(string)})
                .Invoke(patient,new object[]{(float)P(patient,"CurrentHealth")+1f,"wim023:cancel-projection-controlled-death"});
              Require((bool)P(patient,"IsDead"),"Controlled public damage failed");
            }
            phase=1;phaseGame=Time.time;return;
          }
          if(phase==1){
            if((bool)P(order,"IsActive")){
              object[] validate={order,null};
              Require((bool)dt.GetMethod("TryValidate").Invoke(destinations,validate),"Active cancel projection drift:"+validate[1]);
              Require((string)P(order,"replacementExpectedOldPartId")=="surgical-part:1"
                &&(string)P(order,"materialCapacityFingerprint")==fingerprint,"Live authority lost planned old ID");
              return;
            }
            Require(Convert.ToString(P(order,"state"))=="Cancelled"
              &&Convert.ToString(P(order,"materialTerminalDrainPhase"))=="ClosedAwaitingCheckpointGc"
              &&!(bool)P(order,"resultRolled")&&!(bool)P(order,"materialsConsumed")&&!(bool)P(order,"processFluidConsumed")
              &&!(bool)P(order,"anesthesiaConsumed")&&(float)P(order,"completedWork")==0f
              &&string.IsNullOrEmpty((string)P(order,"replacementExpectedOldPartId")),"Terminal cancellation contract failed");
            part=Rows(pt.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null)).Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:2");
            Require(!(bool)P(part,"installed")&&string.IsNullOrEmpty((string)P(part,"reservedOrderId"))
              &&(string)P(part,"physicalItemInstanceId")==physical,"Incoming identity/reservation changed");
            var stocks=Rows(it.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(items,null));
            var cargo=stocks.Where(s=>(string)P(s,"ItemInstanceId")==physical).ToArray();
            Require(cargo.Length==1&&(int)P(cargo[0],"Quantity")==1&&(string)P(cargo[0],"StackId")==stackId
              &&(int)P(cargo[0],"ReservedQuantity")==0&&!stocks.Any(s=>(string)P(s,"DestinationId")==destination),
              "Cancelled cargo lost/duplicated or destination remains owned");
            if(token!=null){
              object[] read={token,null,null};
              Require((bool)admissionType.GetMethod("TryGetPlannedOutputToken").Invoke(admission,read)
                &&Convert.ToString(read[2])=="Released","Aborted output token not Released");
            }
            object[] revoked={order,null};
            Require(!(bool)dt.GetMethod("TryValidate").Invoke(destinations,revoked),"Closed material authority still validates");
            Require(Rows(qt.GetProperty("ActiveOrders").GetValue(query)).Length==0,"Active owner remains");
            settled=JsonUtility.ToJson(order);settledPart=JsonUtility.ToJson(part);phase=2;phaseGame=Time.time;return;
          }
          Require(settled==JsonUtility.ToJson(order)&&settledPart==JsonUtility.ToJson(
            Rows(pt.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null)).Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:2")),
            "Settled cancellation changed on later frame");
          Debug.Log("WIM023 CANCEL_PROJECTION_CASE_PASS index="+caseIndex+";mode="+(caseIndex==2?"death-internal":"public")
            +";initialPhase="+(caseIndex==0?"None":"OutputReserved")+";oldIdClearedAfterClosure=true;incomingExact=true;workRollConsume0=true;laterFrameStable=true"
            +";elapsedGame="+(Time.time-caseGame)+";elapsedReal="+(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-caseReal));
          Debug.Log("WIM023 CANCEL_PROJECTION_ORDER_JSON "+settled);
          if(++caseIndex<3){schedule();return;}
          EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(observerKey,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
          Debug.Log("WIM023 CANCEL_PROJECTION_ALL_PASS cases=3;publicNone/publicReserved/internalDeathReserved;fixtureBootstrapNotActualPositiveRerun=true");
        }catch(Exception ex){
          EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(observerKey,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
          Debug.LogError("WIM023 CANCEL_PROJECTION_ERROR case="+caseIndex+";phase="+phase+";order="+(order==null?"none":JsonUtility.ToJson(order))+";"+ex);
        }
      };
      EditorApplication.update+=tick;Time.timeScale=1f;EditorApplication.isPaused=false;
      result.Log("WIM023 CANCEL_PROJECTION_OBSERVER_STARTED case0;bounded30real/30game each;actualPublicCommands=true");
    }
}
```

#### Executed wim023V4ClinicalNeedsCode
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }




    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result) {
      if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale!=0f
        ||SessionState.GetBool("wim023-observe-surgery:1",false)||SessionState.GetBool("wim023-observe-surgery:2",false))
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Paused no-observer preparation required.");
      var qt=T("ISurgeryQuery");var orders=((IEnumerable)qt.GetProperty("ActiveOrders").GetValue(R(qt))).Cast<object>().ToArray();
      if(orders.Length!=1||(bool)P(orders[0],"resultRolled")||(float)P(orders[0],"completedWork")<=0f||(float)P(orders[0],"completedWork")>=(float)P(orders[0],"requiredWork")||!(bool)P(orders[0],"materialsConsumed"))
        throw new InvalidOperationException("One interrupted unrolled clinical order with preserved partial work required.");
      var pt=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");var parts=((IEnumerable)pt.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(R(pt),null)).Cast<object>().ToArray();
      var selected=parts.Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")==(string)P(orders[0],"selectedPartInstanceId"));
      string key="wim023-clinical-ready-needs:"+P(selected,"physicalItemInstanceId")+":"+P(orders[0],"orderId");
      if(SessionState.GetBool(key,false))throw new InvalidOperationException("This order already received its single need preparation.");
      string[] ids={"owner","character:staff:1:01","character:staff:1:02"};
      var actors=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>()
        .Where(a=>a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&a.gameObject.activeInHierarchy&&P(a,"Identity")!=null
          &&ids.Contains((string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId"))).ToArray();
      if(actors.Length!=3||actors.Select(a=>(string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")).Distinct().Count()!=3
        ||actors.Any(a=>(bool)P(a,"IsDead")||(float)P(a,"CurrentHealth")<=0))
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Three exact living prepared actors required; no resurrection.");
      var st=T("ISurgeryPersistence");var persistence=R(st);
      string surgeryBefore=JsonUtility.ToJson(st.GetMethod("Capture").Invoke(persistence,null));
      SessionState.SetBool(key,true);
      foreach(var actor in actors){
        var stats=P(actor,"Stats");var change=actor.GetType().GetMethod("ChangesStat");
        var ct=change.GetParameters()[0].ParameterType;
        var read=stats.GetType().GetMethod("GetConditionValue",new[]{ct,typeof(float)});
        foreach(string n in new[]{"HUNGER","THIRST","SLEEP","FUN","EXCRETION","HYGIENE"}){
          object condition=Enum.Parse(ct,n);float before=(float)read.Invoke(stats,new object[]{condition,0f});
          change.Invoke(actor,new object[]{condition,100f-before});
          float after=(float)read.Invoke(stats,new object[]{condition,0f});
          if(after<99.99f)throw new InvalidOperationException("Need preparation did not publish:"+n);
          result.Log("WIM023 BOUNDED_NEED_PREPARATION actor={0};need={1};before={2};after={3}",
            P(P(actor,"Identity"),"PersistentId"),n,before,after);
        }
      }
      if(surgeryBefore!=JsonUtility.ToJson(st.GetMethod("Capture").Invoke(persistence,null)))
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Need preparation changed medical aggregate.");
      result.Log("WIM023 THREE_ACTOR_CLINICAL_NEED_PREPARATION_PASS order={0};oncePerOrder=true;decayUnchanged=true;healthUntouched=true;clinicalUntouched=true;setupOnly=true",P(orders[0],"orderId"));
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }




    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result) {
      if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale!=0f
        ||SessionState.GetBool("wim023-observe-surgery:1",false)||SessionState.GetBool("wim023-observe-surgery:2",false))
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Paused no-observer preparation required.");
      var qt=T("ISurgeryQuery");var orders=((IEnumerable)qt.GetProperty("ActiveOrders").GetValue(R(qt))).Cast<object>().ToArray();
      if(orders.Length!=1||(bool)P(orders[0],"resultRolled")||(float)P(orders[0],"completedWork")<0f||(float)P(orders[0],"completedWork")>=(float)P(orders[0],"requiredWork")||(!(bool)P(orders[0],"materialsConsumed")&&Convert.ToString(P(orders[0],"state"))!="Anesthetizing"))
        throw new InvalidOperationException("One unrolled clinical-ready order required; do not recreate materials or progress.");
      var pt=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");var parts=((IEnumerable)pt.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(R(pt),null)).Cast<object>().ToArray();
      var selected=parts.Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")==(string)P(orders[0],"selectedPartInstanceId"));
      string key="wim023-clinical-ready-needs:"+P(selected,"physicalItemInstanceId")+":"+P(orders[0],"orderId");
      if(SessionState.GetBool(key,false))throw new InvalidOperationException("This order already received its single need preparation.");
      string[] ids={"owner","character:staff:1:01","character:staff:1:02"};
      var actors=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>()
        .Where(a=>a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&a.gameObject.activeInHierarchy&&P(a,"Identity")!=null
          &&ids.Contains((string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId"))).ToArray();
      if(actors.Length!=3||actors.Select(a=>(string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")).Distinct().Count()!=3
        ||actors.Any(a=>(bool)P(a,"IsDead")||(float)P(a,"CurrentHealth")<=0))
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Three exact living prepared actors required; no resurrection.");
      var st=T("ISurgeryPersistence");var persistence=R(st);
      string surgeryBefore=JsonUtility.ToJson(st.GetMethod("Capture").Invoke(persistence,null));
      SessionState.SetBool(key,true);
      foreach(var actor in actors){
        var stats=P(actor,"Stats");var change=actor.GetType().GetMethod("ChangesStat");
        var ct=change.GetParameters()[0].ParameterType;
        var read=stats.GetType().GetMethod("GetConditionValue",new[]{ct,typeof(float)});
        foreach(string n in new[]{"HUNGER","THIRST","SLEEP","FUN","EXCRETION","HYGIENE"}){
          object condition=Enum.Parse(ct,n);float before=(float)read.Invoke(stats,new object[]{condition,0f});
          change.Invoke(actor,new object[]{condition,100f-before});
          float after=(float)read.Invoke(stats,new object[]{condition,0f});
          if(after<99.99f)throw new InvalidOperationException("Need preparation did not publish:"+n);
          result.Log("WIM023 BOUNDED_NEED_PREPARATION actor={0};need={1};before={2};after={3}",
            P(P(actor,"Identity"),"PersistentId"),n,before,after);
        }
      }
      if(surgeryBefore!=JsonUtility.ToJson(st.GetMethod("Capture").Invoke(persistence,null)))
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Need preparation changed medical aggregate.");
      result.Log("WIM023 THREE_ACTOR_CLINICAL_NEED_PREPARATION_PASS order={0};oncePerOrder=true;decayUnchanged=true;healthUntouched=true;clinicalUntouched=true;setupOnly=true",P(orders[0],"orderId"));
    }
}
```


### V4 exact-dead lifecycle regression (historical: projection failure; corrected by V5 above)
Run only after positive actual replacement assertion. Third actual Schedule reuses recovered part1. Normal positive game-time scheduler frames establish the living waiting-owner control, public ApplyDamage injects disclosed death, and following scheduler frames must auto-cancel/release exact physical identity/destination without work/roll/consumption/duplication. A later frame must leave settled order/part unchanged. Bound30real/30game seconds. No paused direct Tick, spawn, direct cancellation, natural-mortality or new save/restore claim.
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }



    static void Click(UnityEngine.UI.Button button)
    {
        if(!button.IsInteractable() || UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current==null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual UI input unavailable: "+button.name);
        var pointer=new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerEnterHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerDownHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerUpHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerClickHandler);
    }



    static object[] Rows(object v)=>((IEnumerable)v).Cast<object>().ToArray();
    static void Require(bool ok,string why){if(!ok)throw new InvalidOperationException(why);}
    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result){
      Require(EditorApplication.isPlaying&&EditorApplication.isPaused&&Time.timeScale==0f
        &&!SessionState.GetBool("wim023-observe-surgery:1",false)&&!SessionState.GetBool("wim023-observe-surgery:2",false),"Paused terminal replacement required.");
      var qt=T("ISurgeryQuery");var query=R(qt);object[] previous={"surgery:2",null};
      Require((bool)qt.GetMethod("TryGetOrder").Invoke(query,previous)
        &&Convert.ToString(P(previous[1],"state"))=="Completed"&&(bool)P(previous[1],"resultSucceeded")
        &&Convert.ToString(P(previous[1],"replacementPhase"))=="Completed","Positive actual replacement must precede destructive lifecycle fixture.");
      Require(Rows(qt.GetProperty("ActiveOrders").GetValue(query)).Length==0,"No active orders before single follow-up Schedule.");
      var pt=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");var parts=R(pt);
      var old=Rows(pt.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null)).Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:1");
      Require(!(bool)P(old,"installed")&&string.IsNullOrEmpty((string)P(old,"reservedOrderId")),"Recovered part must be free before follow-up.");
      string physical=(string)P(old,"physicalItemInstanceId");
      var it=T("IWorldItemStackRuntime");var items=R(it);
      var initialStack=Rows(it.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(items,null)).Single(s=>(string)P(s,"ItemInstanceId")==physical);
      string stackId=(string)P(initialStack,"StackId");
      string priorDestination=(string)P(initialStack,"DestinationId");
      Require(string.IsNullOrEmpty(priorDestination),"Recovered source must be unassigned before scheduler dispatch.");
      var view=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterSurgeryWindowView")).OfType<Component>()
        .Single(c=>c.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&c.gameObject.activeInHierarchy).transform;
      Click(view.Find("SurgeryPanel/ProcedureRow/Previous").GetComponent<UnityEngine.UI.Button>());
      Click(view.Find("SurgeryPanel/ProcedureRow/Next").GetComponent<UnityEngine.UI.Button>());
      Click(view.Find("SurgeryPanel/Commands/Schedule").GetComponent<UnityEngine.UI.Button>());
      var active=Rows(qt.GetProperty("ActiveOrders").GetValue(query));
      Require(active.Length==1,"Exactly one follow-up order required.");
      var order=active[0];
      Require((string)P(order,"orderId")=="surgery:3"&&(string)P(order,"selectedPartInstanceId")=="surgical-part:1"
        &&(string)P(P(order,"subject"),"subjectId")=="character:staff:1:01"&&!(bool)P(order,"resultRolled"),"Follow-up order identity mismatch.");
      var patient=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>()
        .Single(a=>a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&a.gameObject.activeInHierarchy&&(string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")=="character:staff:1:01");
      Require(!(bool)P(patient,"IsDead"),"Living patient required before fault injection.");

      string destination=(string)P(order,"materialDestinationId");
      const string observerKey="wim023-death-cleanup-observer";
      Require(!SessionState.GetBool(observerKey,false),"Death observer already active.");
      double start=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;float startGame=Time.time,phaseGame=Time.time;
      int phase=0;string settledOrder=null,settledPart=null;
      SessionState.SetBool(observerKey,true);
      EditorApplication.CallbackFunction tick=null;
      tick=()=>{
        try{
          Require(EditorApplication.isPlaying,"Temporary world ended while death observer active.");
          Require(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start<30d&&Time.time-startGame<30f,"Bounded death cleanup not complete.");
          if(EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale<=0f||Time.time<=phaseGame)return;
          if(phase==0){
            Require((bool)P(order,"IsActive")&&(bool)P(order,"OwnsMaterialAuthority")
              &&!(bool)P(order,"resultRolled")&&!(bool)P(order,"materialsConsumed"),"Live waiting owner invalid before death.");
            old=Rows(pt.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null)).Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:1");
            Require((string)P(old,"reservedOrderId")=="surgery:3","Selected part not reserved.");
            patient.GetType().GetMethod("ApplyDamage",new[]{typeof(float),typeof(string)})
              .Invoke(patient,new object[]{(float)P(patient,"CurrentHealth")+1f,"wim023:controlled-death-cleanup-fixture"});
            Require((bool)P(patient,"IsDead"),"Production vital damage did not mark death.");
            phase=1;phaseGame=Time.time;
            Debug.Log("WIM023 CONTROLLED_DEATH_INJECTED livingOwnerVerified=true;game="+Time.time);
            return;
          }
          if(phase==1){
            if(Convert.ToString(P(order,"state"))!="Cancelled")return;
            Require(!(bool)P(order,"IsActive")
              &&Convert.ToString(P(order,"materialTerminalDrainPhase"))=="ClosedAwaitingCheckpointGc"
              &&!Rows(qt.GetProperty("ActiveOrders").GetValue(query)).Any(o=>(string)P(o,"facilityId")==(string)P(order,"facilityId"))
              &&!(bool)P(order,"resultRolled")&&!(bool)P(order,"materialsConsumed")&&(float)P(order,"completedWork")==0f,
              "Dead order retained ownership or consumed/rolled.");
            old=Rows(pt.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null)).Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:1");
            Require(!(bool)P(old,"installed")&&string.IsNullOrEmpty((string)P(old,"reservedOrderId"))
              &&(string)P(old,"physicalItemInstanceId")==physical,"Death cleanup lost or retained selected part.");
            var after=Rows(it.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(items,null)).Where(s=>(string)P(s,"ItemInstanceId")==physical).ToArray();
            Require(after.Length==1&&(int)P(after[0],"Quantity")==1&&(string)P(after[0],"StackId")==stackId
              &&(int)P(after[0],"ReservedQuantity")==0&&(string)P(after[0],"DestinationId")==priorDestination,
              "Selected physical cargo lost/duplicated or wrong destination.");
            Require(!Rows(it.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(items,null)).Any(s=>(string)P(s,"DestinationId")==destination),
              "Closed destination still owns stock.");
            settledOrder=JsonUtility.ToJson(order);settledPart=JsonUtility.ToJson(old);
            phase=2;phaseGame=Time.time;return;
          }
          Require(settledOrder==JsonUtility.ToJson(order)
            &&settledPart==JsonUtility.ToJson(Rows(pt.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null)).Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:1")),
            "Later scheduler frame changed settled order/part.");
          EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(observerKey,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
          Debug.Log("WIM023 DEAD_PATIENT_AUTO_CANCEL_PASS livingOwnerBefore=true;deadCancelled=true;order=surgery:3;part="+physical
            +";stack="+stackId+";quantity1;reserved0;priorDestinationExact=true;closedDestinationEmpty=true;roll0;work0;consumption0;laterFrameIdempotent=true;controlledDeathFixture=true;gameElapsed="+(Time.time-startGame)+";realElapsed="+(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start));
          Debug.Log("WIM023 DEAD_ORDER_JSON "+settledOrder);
        }catch(Exception ex){
          EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(observerKey,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
          Debug.LogError("WIM023 DEATH_CLEANUP_ERROR "+ex);
        }
      };
      EditorApplication.update+=tick;Time.timeScale=1f;EditorApplication.isPaused=false;
      result.Log("WIM023 DEATH_OBSERVER_STARTED positiveSchedulerFramesOnly=true;maxReal30;maxGame30;initialGame={0};initialReal={1}",startGame,start);
    }
}
```

### V4 corrected bounded preparation (root-owned, NOT_RUN)
Previous V3 patient death is invalid positive clinical evidence; no resurrection/replay in that world. Protected Stop223282.4674993 preserved five user files. Fresh setup is needed because there is no world checkpoint after first installation. First-install scoped PASS remains closed.
Give six non-mood needs through public ChangesStat exactly once to all three living actors per prepared order; medical aggregate must remain byte-identical. No health/decay/RNG change. When actual clinical work becomes eligible, issue the existing public typed Surgery work command once, then only observe actual AI. Bound one game day/90 real seconds; failure is diagnostic, not an excuse to extend/reseed/regrant. Generic multi-work Staff UI selection is not claimed.
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");

            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });

    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result) {
      if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale!=0f
        ||SessionState.GetBool("wim023-observe-surgery:1",false)||SessionState.GetBool("wim023-observe-surgery:2",false))

      var qt=T("ISurgeryQuery");var orders=((IEnumerable)qt.GetProperty("ActiveOrders").GetValue(R(qt))).Cast<object>().ToArray();

      var selected=parts.Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")==(string)P(orders[0],"selectedPartInstanceId"));

        .Where(a=>a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&a.gameObject.activeInHierarchy&&P(a,"Identity")!=null
          &&ids.Contains((string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId"))).ToArray();
      if(actors.Length!=3||actors.Select(a=>(string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")).Distinct().Count()!=3
        ||actors.Any(a=>(bool)P(a,"IsDead")||(float)P(a,"CurrentHealth")<=0))
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Three exact living prepared actors required; no resurrection.");

        var read=stats.GetType().GetMethod("GetConditionValue",new[]{ct,typeof(float)});
        foreach(string n in new[]{"HUNGER","THIRST","SLEEP","FUN","EXCRETION","HYGIENE"}){
          object condition=Enum.Parse(ct,n);float before=(float)read.Invoke(stats,new object[]{condition,0f});
          change.Invoke(actor,new object[]{condition,100f-before});
          float after=(float)read.Invoke(stats,new object[]{condition,0f});
          if(after<99.99f)throw new InvalidOperationException("Need preparation did not publish:"+n);
          result.Log("WIM023 BOUNDED_NEED_PREPARATION actor={0};need={1};before={2};after={3}",
            P(P(actor,"Identity"),"PersistentId"),n,before,after);
        }
      }
      if(surgeryBefore!=JsonUtility.ToJson(st.GetMethod("Capture").Invoke(persistence,null)))
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Need preparation changed medical aggregate.");
      result.Log("WIM023 THREE_ACTOR_NEED_PREPARATION_PASS order={0};oncePerOrder=true;decayUnchanged=true;healthUntouched=true;clinicalUntouched=true;setupOnly=true",P(orders[0],"orderId"));
    }
```

#### Observer: first order (second substitutes only order/part IDs)
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }



    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale!=0f)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected paused surgery required.");
        const string key="wim023-observe-surgery:1";
        if(SessionState.GetBool(key,false))throw new InvalidOperationException("Observer already registered; inspect existing run.");
        var type=T("ISurgeryQuery");object query=R(type);var get=type.GetMethod("TryGetOrder");
        object[] initial={"surgery:1",null};
        if(!(bool)get.Invoke(query,initial)||(string)P(initial[1],"selectedPartInstanceId")!="surgical-part:1"
          ||Convert.ToString(P(initial[1],"state"))=="Completed")throw new InvalidOperationException("Expected first order unavailable.");
        var partType=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");
        var part=((IEnumerable)partType.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(R(partType),null)).Cast<object>()
            .Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:1");
        string selectedStack=(string)P(part,"worldStackId"),selectedInstance=(string)P(part,"physicalItemInstanceId");
        if(!SessionState.GetBool("wim023-ready-needs:"+selectedInstance+":surgery:1",false))
          throw new InvalidOperationException("Matching once-per-order need preparation missing.");
        var itemType=T("IWorldItemStackRuntime");object items=R(itemType);bool sawRoutable=false;
        
        var doctor=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>()
          .Single(a=>a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&a.gameObject.activeInHierarchy&&(string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")=="owner");
        var patient=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>()
          .Single(a=>a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&a.gameObject.activeInHierarchy&&(string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")=="character:staff:1:01");
        var table=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("BuildableObject")).OfType<Component>()
          .Single(f=>f.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&Convert.ToString(P(f,"PersistentInstanceId"))==(string)P(initial[1],"facilityId"));
        var workAbility=doctor.GetType().GetMethods().Single(m=>m.Name=="GetAbility"&&m.IsGenericMethodDefinition)
          .MakeGenericMethod(T("AbilityWork")).Invoke(doctor,null);
        var assign=workAbility.GetType().GetMethods().Single(m=>m.Name=="TrySetPriorityWorkTarget"&&m.GetParameters().Length==4&&m.GetParameters()[1].ParameterType.Name=="WorkTypeId");
        object surgeryId=Activator.CreateInstance(assign.GetParameters()[1].ParameterType,new object[]{"work:surgery"});
        bool preparedJob=false;

        double start=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;float gameStart=Time.time;int last=-1;
        SessionState.SetBool(key,true);
        EditorApplication.CallbackFunction tick=null;
        tick=()=>{
          try{
            if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying)throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected Play ended during observation.");
            object[] args={"surgery:1",null};
            if(!(bool)get.Invoke(query,args))throw new InvalidOperationException("Observed order disappeared.");
            object order=args[1];
            if((bool)P(patient,"IsDead"))throw new InvalidOperationException("PREPARATION_PATIENT_DIED");
            if(!preparedJob){
              object[] candidate={table,null};object[] eligibility={order,doctor,null};
              if((bool)type.GetMethod("TryGetWorkFor").Invoke(query,candidate)
                &&(string)P(candidate[1],"orderId")==(string)P(order,"orderId")
                &&(bool)type.GetMethod("CanOperate").Invoke(query,eligibility)){
                var brain=P(doctor,"Brain");object search=brain.GetType().GetMethod("GetPathSearch").Invoke(brain,new object[]{doctor});
                object[] command={table,surgeryId,search,null};
                if(!(bool)assign.Invoke(workAbility,command))
                  throw new InvalidOperationException("PREPARATION_EXPLICIT_JOB_REJECTED:"+command[3]);
                if(Convert.ToString(P(workAbility,"AssignedWorkTypeId"))!="work:surgery"
                  ||!ReferenceEquals(P(workAbility,"PriorityWorkTarget"),table))
                  throw new InvalidOperationException("PREPARATION_JOB_NOT_PUBLISHED");
                preparedJob=true;
                Debug.Log("WIM023 TYPED_JOB_PREPARATION_PASS order="+P(order,"orderId")+";actualWorkNotForced=true;game="+Time.time);
              }
            }

            int window=(int)((Time.time-gameStart)/30f);
            bool terminal=!(bool)P(order,"IsActive");
            bool expired=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start>=90d||Time.time-gameStart>=180f;
            if(window!=last||terminal||expired){
              last=window;
              if(!sawRoutable){
                var routed=((IEnumerable)itemType.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(items,null)).Cast<object>()
                    .SingleOrDefault(s=>(string)P(s,"StackId")==selectedStack);
                if(routed!=null&&(string)P(routed,"DestinationId")==(string)P(order,"materialDestinationId")){
                  if(Convert.ToString(P(routed,"State"))=="FacilityOutputBuffer"
                    ||(int)P(routed,"Quantity")!=1||(string)P(routed,"ItemInstanceId")!=selectedInstance)
                      throw new InvalidOperationException("Selected output retarget state/identity/quantity invalid.");
                  sawRoutable=true;
                  Debug.Log("WIM023 SELECTED_OUTPUT_ROUTABLE_PASS stack="+selectedStack+";instance="+selectedInstance+";state="+P(routed,"State")+";quantity=1;position="+P(routed,"Position"));
                }
              }
              Debug.Log("WIM023 AI_WINDOW order=surgery:1;state="+P(order,"state")+";work="+P(order,"completedWork")+"/"+P(order,"requiredWork")
                +";doctor="+P(order,"doctorId")+";patientAdmitted="+P(order,"patientAdmitted")+";materialConsumed="+P(order,"materialsConsumed")
                +";admission="+P(order,"admissionX")+","+P(order,"admissionY")+";status="+P(P(order,"statusData"),"code")+";gameElapsed="+(Time.time-gameStart)+";realElapsed="+(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start));
            }
            if(terminal||expired){
              EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(key,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
              Debug.Log("WIM023 AI_OBSERVER_TERMINAL cause="+(terminal?"ORDER_TERMINAL":"BOUNDED_OBSERVATION_END")+";state="+P(order,"state")+";result="+P(order,"resultSucceeded"));
            }
          }catch(Exception ex){
            EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(key,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
            Debug.LogError("WIM023 AI_OBSERVER_ERROR "+ex);
          }
        };
        EditorApplication.update+=tick;Time.timeScale=8f;EditorApplication.isPaused=false;
        result.Log("WIM023 AI_OBSERVER_STARTED order=surgery:1;maxReal=90;maxGame=180;scale=8;initialGame={0};initialReal={1};actualWorkOnly=true",gameStart,start);
    }
}
```

### Explicit work-type preparation (root-owned)
Generic resolver refusal is preserved. Existing public AbilityWork.TrySetPriorityWorkTarget(M03,work:surgery,Brain.GetPathSearch(owner),out reason) performs its own emergency/access/assignment checks. This is an explicit player-work backend preparation, not a direct surgery-work call or new UI proof. No further need grant.
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }



    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result){
      if(!EditorApplication.isPaused||SessionState.GetBool("wim023-observe-surgery:2",false))throw new InvalidOperationException("Paused terminal required.");
      var qt=T("ISurgeryQuery");var q=R(qt);object[] oa={"surgery:2",null};qt.GetMethod("TryGetOrder").Invoke(q,oa);
      var order=oa[1];
      var owner=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>()
        .Single(a=>a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&a.gameObject.activeInHierarchy&&(string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")=="owner");
      var table=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("BuildableObject")).OfType<Component>()
        .Single(f=>f.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&Convert.ToString(P(f,"PersistentInstanceId"))==(string)P(order,"facilityId"));
      object[] work={table,null};bool available=(bool)qt.GetMethod("TryGetWorkFor").Invoke(q,work);
      object[] eligibility={order,owner,null};bool eligible=(bool)qt.GetMethod("CanOperate").Invoke(q,eligibility);
      result.Log("WIM023 CLINICAL_QUERY available={0};order={1};ownerEligible={2};failure={3};actorCanRun={4};actorPaused={5}",
        available,available?P(work[1],"orderId"):"",eligible,eligibility[2],P(owner,"CanRunAi"),owner.GetType().GetMethod("IsAiPaused").Invoke(owner,null));
      var wa=owner.GetType().GetMethods().Single(m=>m.Name=="GetAbility"&&m.IsGenericMethodDefinition).MakeGenericMethod(T("AbilityWork")).Invoke(owner,null);
      if(!available||!eligible||(bool)P(order,"resultRolled")||(float)P(order,"completedWork")!=0f)
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Current qualified unrolled handoff required.");
      var assign=wa.GetType().GetMethods().Single(m=>m.Name=="TrySetPriorityWorkTarget"
        &&m.GetParameters().Length==4&&m.GetParameters()[1].ParameterType.Name=="WorkTypeId");
      object surgeryId=Activator.CreateInstance(assign.GetParameters()[1].ParameterType,new object[]{"work:surgery"});
      var brain=P(owner,"Brain");
      object search=brain.GetType().GetMethod("GetPathSearch").Invoke(brain,new object[]{owner});
      object[] issued={table,surgeryId,search,null};
      if(!(bool)assign.Invoke(wa,issued))throw new InvalidOperationException("Explicit public priority-work rejected:"+issued[3]);
      if(!ReferenceEquals(P(wa,"PriorityWorkTarget"),table)||Convert.ToString(P(wa,"PriorityWorkTypeId"))!="work:surgery"
        ||Convert.ToString(P(wa,"AssignedWorkTypeId"))!="work:surgery")
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Explicit command failed to replace guard assignment.");
      result.Log("WIM023 EXPLICIT_SURGERY_JOB_PREPARATION_PASS target={0};job=surgery;message={1};normalPathValidation=true;noApplyWork=true;noRngReset=true;needGrantNotRepeated=true",
        P(table,"PersistentInstanceId"),issued[3]);

    }
}
```

### Disclosed normal player job preparation — no new production change

The root uses the same public IPlayerStaffCommandSource command as StaffManagementSurfacePanel's surgery-table priority card, not direct surgery reservation/work. This is preparation via player command (not a new Staff UI proof); existing actual Schedule UI and all actual clinical/haul AI remain required. No repeat need grants/RNG seed/parts/first surgery.
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }



    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result){
      if(!EditorApplication.isPaused||SessionState.GetBool("wim023-observe-surgery:2",false))throw new InvalidOperationException("Paused terminal required.");
      var qt=T("ISurgeryQuery");var q=R(qt);object[] oa={"surgery:2",null};qt.GetMethod("TryGetOrder").Invoke(q,oa);
      var order=oa[1];
      var owner=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>()
        .Single(a=>a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&a.gameObject.activeInHierarchy&&(string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")=="owner");
      var table=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("BuildableObject")).OfType<Component>()
        .Single(f=>f.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&Convert.ToString(P(f,"PersistentInstanceId"))==(string)P(order,"facilityId"));
      object[] work={table,null};bool available=(bool)qt.GetMethod("TryGetWorkFor").Invoke(q,work);
      object[] eligibility={order,owner,null};bool eligible=(bool)qt.GetMethod("CanOperate").Invoke(q,eligibility);
      result.Log("WIM023 CLINICAL_QUERY available={0};order={1};ownerEligible={2};failure={3};actorCanRun={4};actorPaused={5}",
        available,available?P(work[1],"orderId"):"",eligible,eligibility[2],P(owner,"CanRunAi"),owner.GetType().GetMethod("IsAiPaused").Invoke(owner,null));
      var wa=owner.GetType().GetMethods().Single(m=>m.Name=="GetAbility"&&m.IsGenericMethodDefinition).MakeGenericMethod(T("AbilityWork")).Invoke(owner,null);
      object[] resolution={owner,table,null,null};
      if(!(bool)T("WorkCommandResolver").GetMethod("TryResolveFacilityCommand").Invoke(null,resolution)
        ||Convert.ToString(resolution[2])!="work:surgery")
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Existing player command must resolve exactly surgery:"+resolution[3]);
      if(!available||!eligible||(bool)P(order,"resultRolled")||(float)P(order,"completedWork")!=0f)
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Current unrolled qualified surgery handoff required.");
      var ct=T("IPlayerStaffCommandSource");var commands=R(ct);
      object[] selected={owner,null};
      if(!(bool)ct.GetMethod("TrySelectActor").Invoke(commands,selected))throw new InvalidOperationException("Player selection failed:"+selected[1]);
      object[] issued={table,null};
      if(!(bool)ct.GetMethod("TryIssuePriorityWorkCommand").Invoke(commands,issued))throw new InvalidOperationException("Player work command failed:"+issued[1]);
      if(!ReferenceEquals(P(wa,"PriorityWorkTarget"),table)||Convert.ToString(P(wa,"PriorityWorkTypeId"))!="work:surgery"
        ||Convert.ToString(P(wa,"AssignedWorkTypeId"))!="work:surgery")
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual player command did not replace guard with surgical target.");
      result.Log("WIM023 DISCLOSED_PLAYER_JOB_COMMAND_PASS actor=owner;old=guard;new=surgery;target={0};message={1};noApplyWork=true;noRngReset=true;needCorrectionNotRepeated=true",
        P(table,"PersistentInstanceId"),issued[1]);

    }
}
```

### Executable need-only preparation with exact carry ownership guard
Root incorporated Sol017 review: ownerCharacterId, world owner destination, sole intent commitment and current owner carry entry join exact stack/instance/operation/quantity before mutation. Normal derived mood recalculation is expected; no direct MOOD assignment.
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }



    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result){
      if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||SessionState.GetBool("wim023-observe-surgery:2",false))
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Paused terminal second-surgery world required.");
      var actors=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>()
        .Where(c=>c.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&c.gameObject.activeInHierarchy
          &&((string)P(P(c,"Identity"),"PersistentId")=="owner"||(string)P(P(c,"Identity"),"PersistentId")=="character:staff:1:02")).ToArray();
      if(actors.Length!=2)throw new InvalidOperationException("Expected existing doctor and hauler.");
      var qt=T("ISurgeryQuery");object[] oa={"surgery:2",null};
      if(!(bool)qt.GetMethod("TryGetOrder").Invoke(R(qt),oa)
        ||Convert.ToString(P(oa[1],"state"))!="MaterialsWaiting"||(bool)P(oa[1],"resultRolled")
        ||(float)P(oa[1],"completedWork")!=0f)
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Unrolled waiting order expected.");
      string orderBefore=JsonUtility.ToJson(oa[1]);
      var owner=actors.Single(a=>(string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")=="owner");
      var haul=owner.GetComponent(T("AbilityHaul"));
      var capture=haul.GetType().GetMethod("CaptureDeliveryIntentForSave");
      var intent=capture.Invoke(haul,null);string intentBefore=JsonUtility.ToJson(intent);
      if(intent==null||(string)P(intent,"destinationId")!="surgery-materials:surgery:2")
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Preserved second-surgery delivery intent absent.");
      var stacksType=T("IWorldItemStackRuntime");var stacks=R(stacksType);
      var before=((IEnumerable)stacksType.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(stacks,null)).Cast<object>()
        .Single(s=>(string)P(s,"ItemInstanceId")==SessionState.GetString("wim023-second-physical",""));
      if(Convert.ToString(P(before,"State"))!="Carried"||(int)P(before,"Quantity")!=1)
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Expected original retained carried part2.");
      var commitments=((IEnumerable)P(intent,"commitments")).Cast<object>().ToArray();
      var carry=((IEnumerable)P(P(owner,"CarryInventory"),"Items")).Cast<object>()
        .Where(e=>(string)P(e,"carriedStackId")==(string)P(before,"StackId")).ToArray();
      if((string)P(intent,"ownerCharacterId")!="owner"||(string)P(before,"DestinationId")!="owner"
        ||commitments.Length!=1||(string)P(commitments[0],"carriedStackId")!=(string)P(before,"StackId")
        ||(string)P(commitments[0],"itemId")!=(string)P(before,"ItemId")||(int)P(commitments[0],"quantity")!=1
        ||carry.Length!=1||(string)P(carry[0],"itemInstanceId")!=(string)P(before,"ItemInstanceId")
        ||(string)P(carry[0],"ownerOperationId")!=(string)P(intent,"operationId")||(int)P(carry[0],"quantity")!=1)
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Exact owner intent/carry/world physical commitment mismatch.");
      var pt=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");string partsBefore=string.Join("|",((IEnumerable)pt.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(R(pt),null)).Cast<object>().Select(p=>JsonUtility.ToJson(p)));
      foreach(var actor in actors){
        object stats=P(actor,"Stats");
        var change=actor.GetType().GetMethod("ChangesStat");Type conditionType=change.GetParameters()[0].ParameterType;
        var read=stats.GetType().GetMethod("GetConditionValue",new[]{conditionType,typeof(float)});
        foreach(string name in new[]{"HUNGER","THIRST","SLEEP","FUN","EXCRETION","HYGIENE"}){
          object condition=Enum.Parse(conditionType,name);float previous=(float)read.Invoke(stats,new object[]{condition,0f});
          actor.GetType().GetMethod("ChangesStat").Invoke(actor,new object[]{condition,100f-previous});
          float after=(float)read.Invoke(stats,new object[]{condition,0f});
          if(after!=100f)throw new InvalidOperationException("Disclosed need preparation failed:"+name);
          result.Log("WIM023 DISCLOSED_NEED_PREPARATION actor={0};need={1};before={2};after={3};onceOnly=true",
            P(P(actor,"Identity"),"PersistentId"),name,previous,after);
        }
      }
      if(JsonUtility.ToJson(capture.Invoke(haul,null))!=intentBefore||JsonUtility.ToJson(oa[1])!=orderBefore
        ||string.Join("|",((IEnumerable)pt.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(R(pt),null)).Cast<object>().Select(p=>JsonUtility.ToJson(p)))!=partsBefore)
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Need fixture changed surgery/part/haul authority.");
      result.Log("WIM023 NEED_PREPARATION_READY noClinicalWork=true;noRngReset=true;noPartOrIntentMutation=true;survivalBalanceNotVerified=true");
    }
}
```

### Disclosed one-time need preparation for interrupted second haul — pending execution

### Root actual replacement final assertion — not yet run
This checks exact original/new physical identities, actual body and receipt joins, one recovered stack, zero installed-new world stack, detached durability and current-format Capture. Existing18/18 restore proof is retained; this capture is not described as a new restore test.
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }



    static object[] Rows(object v)=>((IEnumerable)v).Cast<object>().ToArray();
    static void Require(bool value,string reason){if(!value)throw new InvalidOperationException(reason);}
    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result) {
      Require(EditorApplication.isPlaying&&EditorApplication.isPaused&&Time.timeScale==0f
        &&!SessionState.GetBool("wim023-observe-surgery:2",false),"Replacement observer must be terminal and paused.");
      string oldId=SessionState.GetString("wim023-first-installed-physical","");
      string newId=SessionState.GetString("wim023-second-physical","");
      Require(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(oldId)&&!string.IsNullOrEmpty(newId)&&oldId!=newId,"Prior physical identity evidence missing.");
      var qt=T("ISurgeryQuery");object[] oa={"surgery:2",null};
      Require((bool)qt.GetMethod("TryGetOrder").Invoke(R(qt),oa),"Replacement order missing.");
      var order=oa[1];
      Require(Convert.ToString(P(order,"state"))=="Completed"&&(bool)P(order,"resultSucceeded")
        &&(float)P(order,"completedWork")==(float)P(order,"requiredWork")
        &&(bool)P(order,"materialsConsumed")&&(bool)P(order,"processFluidConsumed")&&(bool)P(order,"anesthesiaConsumed"),
        "Replacement clinical/material completion incomplete.");
      Require(Convert.ToString(P(order,"replacementPhase"))=="Completed"
        &&(string)P(order,"replacementExpectedOldPartId")=="surgical-part:1"
        &&(string)P(order,"replacementIncomingPartId")=="surgical-part:2"
        &&(string)P(order,"replacementOutputItemInstanceId")==oldId,
        "Replacement phase/old/new/output identity mismatch.");
      var pt=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");var parts=Rows(pt.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(R(pt),null));
      var old=parts.Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:1");
      var incoming=parts.Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:2");
      Require(!(bool)P(old,"installed")&&string.IsNullOrEmpty((string)P(old,"installedSubjectId"))
        &&(string)P(old,"physicalItemInstanceId")==oldId&&(string)P(old,"recoveryOrderId")=="surgery:2"
        &&!string.IsNullOrEmpty((string)P(old,"recoveryCommitId"))
        &&(string)P(old,"recoveryCommitId")==(string)P(order,"replacementBatchCommitId")
        &&(string)P(old,"recoveryOperationId")==(string)P(order,"replacementOperationId"),
        "Detached original part ownership/recovery receipt mismatch.");
      Require((bool)P(incoming,"installed")&&(string)P(incoming,"installedSubjectId")=="character:staff:1:01"
        &&(string)P(incoming,"physicalItemInstanceId")==newId
        &&(string)P(incoming,"installationOrderId")=="surgery:2"
        &&!string.IsNullOrEmpty((string)P(incoming,"installationCommitId")),
        "Incoming part installed ownership/receipt mismatch.");
      var at=T("IAnatomyHealthRuntime");
      var body=at.GetMethod("GetAnatomySnapshot",new[]{typeof(string)}).Invoke(R(at),new object[]{"character:staff:1:01"});
      var arm=Rows(P(body,"Nodes")).Single(n=>(string)P(n,"nodeId")=="arm:left");
      Require((string)P(arm,"installedPartId")=="surgical-part:2","Actual body still owns original part.");
      var it=T("IWorldItemStackRuntime");var stacks=Rows(it.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(R(it),null));
      var oldStacks=stacks.Where(s=>(string)P(s,"ItemInstanceId")==oldId).ToArray();
      Require(oldStacks.Length==1&&(int)P(oldStacks[0],"Quantity")==1
        &&!stacks.Any(s=>(string)P(s,"ItemInstanceId")==newId),"Recovered original lost/duplicated or installed new part still physical.");
      Require((string)P(oldStacks[0],"StackId")==(string)P(old,"worldStackId")
        &&(string)P(old,"worldStackId")==(string)P(order,"replacementOutputStackId")
        &&(float)P(old,"detachedDurabilityCurrent")==(float)P(order,"replacementDetachedCurrentHealth")
        &&(float)P(old,"detachedDurabilityMaximum")==(float)P(order,"replacementDetachedMaxHealth"),
        "Recovered stack/detached durability join mismatch.");
      var st=T("ISurgeryPersistence");var save=st.GetMethod("Capture").Invoke(R(st),null);
      var savedOrder=Rows(P(save,"orders")).Single(o=>(string)P(o,"orderId")=="surgery:2");
      var savedOld=Rows(P(save,"parts")).Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:1");
      var savedNew=Rows(P(save,"parts")).Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:2");
      Require((string)P(savedOrder,"replacementOutputItemInstanceId")==oldId
        &&(string)P(savedOld,"physicalItemInstanceId")==oldId&&!(bool)P(savedOld,"installed")
        &&(string)P(savedOld,"recoveryCommitId")==(string)P(old,"recoveryCommitId")
        &&(string)P(savedNew,"physicalItemInstanceId")==newId&&(bool)P(savedNew,"installed"),
        "Current-format surgery capture lost actual replacement joins.");
      result.Log("WIM023 SECOND_ACTUAL_REPLACEMENT_RECOVERY_PASS old={0};new={1};oldStack={2};oldQuantity=1;newStack0;body=part2;oldState={3};oldPosition={4};recoveryCommit={5};saveCaptureJoin=true;restoreNotRepeated=true",
        oldId,newId,P(oldStacks[0],"StackId"),P(oldStacks[0],"State"),P(oldStacks[0],"Position"),P(old,"recoveryCommitId"));
      result.Log("WIM023 FINAL_ORDER_JSON {0}",JsonUtility.ToJson(order));
      result.Log("WIM023 RECOVERED_PART_JSON {0}",JsonUtility.ToJson(old));
      result.Log("WIM023 INSTALLED_PART_JSON {0}",JsonUtility.ToJson(incoming));
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;

using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }


    static void Click(UnityEngine.UI.Button button)
    {
        if(!button.IsInteractable() || UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current==null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual UI input unavailable: "+button.name);
        var pointer=new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerEnterHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerDownHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerUpHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerClickHandler);
    }


    static object[] Rows(object o)=>((IEnumerable)o).Cast<object>().ToArray();
    static void SetPriority(object actor, string workId)
    {
        var ability=actor.GetType().GetMethods().Single(m=>m.Name=="GetAbility"&&m.IsGenericMethodDefinition)
            .MakeGenericMethod(T("AbilityWork")).Invoke(actor,null);

        var duty=ability.GetType().GetMethod("SetDutyState");
        duty.Invoke(ability,new object[]{Enum.Parse(duty.GetParameters()[0].ParameterType,"OnDuty")});
    }

            throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected paused setup required.");

            throw new InvalidOperationException("Existing active surgery; inspect, do not replay Schedule.");
```
