```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }



    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale!=0f)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected paused surgery required.");
        const string key="wim023-observe-surgery:2";
        if(SessionState.GetBool(key,false))throw new InvalidOperationException("Observer already registered; inspect existing run.");
        var type=T("ISurgeryQuery");object query=R(type);var get=type.GetMethod("TryGetOrder");
        object[] initial={"surgery:2",null};
        if(!(bool)get.Invoke(query,initial)||(string)P(initial[1],"selectedPartInstanceId")!="surgical-part:2"
          ||Convert.ToString(P(initial[1],"state"))=="Completed")throw new InvalidOperationException("Expected first order unavailable.");
        var partType=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");
        var part=((IEnumerable)partType.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(R(partType),null)).Cast<object>()
            .Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:2");
        string selectedStack=(string)P(part,"worldStackId"),selectedInstance=(string)P(part,"physicalItemInstanceId");
        var itemType=T("IWorldItemStackRuntime");object items=R(itemType);bool sawRoutable=false;
        double start=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;float gameStart=Time.time;int last=-1;
        SessionState.SetBool(key,true);
        EditorApplication.CallbackFunction tick=null;
        tick=()=>{
          try{
            if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying)throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected Play ended during observation.");
            object[] args={"surgery:2",null};
            if(!(bool)get.Invoke(query,args))throw new InvalidOperationException("Observed order disappeared.");
            object order=args[1];int window=(int)((Time.time-gameStart)/30f);
            bool terminal=!(bool)P(order,"IsActive");
            bool expired=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start>=150d||Time.time-gameStart>=360f;
            if(window!=last||terminal||expired){
              last=window;
              if(!sawRoutable){
                var routed=((IEnumerable)itemType.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(items,null)).Cast<object>()
                    .SingleOrDefault(s=>(string)P(s,"StackId")==selectedStack);
                if(routed!=null&&(string)P(routed,"DestinationId")==(string)P(order,"materialDestinationId")){
                  if(Convert.ToString(P(routed,"State"))=="FacilityOutputBuffer"
                    ||(int)P(routed,"Quantity")!=1||(string)P(routed,"ItemInstanceId")!=selectedInstance)
                      throw new InvalidOperationException("Selected output retarget state/identity/quantity invalid.");
                  sawRoutable=true;
                  Debug.Log("WIM023 SELECTED_OUTPUT_ROUTABLE_PASS stack="+selectedStack+";instance="+selectedInstance+";state="+P(routed,"State")+";quantity=1;position="+P(routed,"Position"));
                }
              }
              Debug.Log("WIM023 AI_WINDOW order=surgery:2;state="+P(order,"state")+";work="+P(order,"completedWork")+"/"+P(order,"requiredWork")
                +";doctor="+P(order,"doctorId")+";patientAdmitted="+P(order,"patientAdmitted")+";materialConsumed="+P(order,"materialsConsumed")
                +";admission="+P(order,"admissionX")+","+P(order,"admissionY")+";status="+P(P(order,"statusData"),"code")+";gameElapsed="+(Time.time-gameStart)+";realElapsed="+(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start));
            }
            if(terminal||expired){
              EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(key,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
              Debug.Log("WIM023 AI_OBSERVER_TERMINAL cause="+(terminal?"ORDER_TERMINAL":"BOUNDED_OBSERVATION_END")+";state="+P(order,"state")+";result="+P(order,"resultSucceeded"));
            }
          }catch(Exception ex){
            EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(key,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
            Debug.LogError("WIM023 AI_OBSERVER_ERROR "+ex);
          }
        };
        EditorApplication.update+=tick;Time.timeScale=8f;EditorApplication.isPaused=false;
        result.Log("WIM023 AI_OBSERVER_STARTED order=surgery:2;maxReal=150;maxGame=360;scale=8;initialGame={0};initialReal={1};actualWorkOnly=true",gameStart,start);
    }
}
```

### Current first actual installation identity assertion (root-owned)
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }



    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result) {
      if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||SessionState.GetBool("wim023-observe-surgery:1",false))
          throw new InvalidOperationException("First observer must be terminal and paused.");
      var queryType=T("ISurgeryQuery");object[] args={"surgery:1",null};
      if(!(bool)queryType.GetMethod("TryGetOrder").Invoke(R(queryType),args))throw new InvalidOperationException("First order missing.");
      object order=args[1];
      if(Convert.ToString(P(order,"state"))!="Completed"||!(bool)P(order,"resultSucceeded")
        ||(float)P(order,"completedWork")!=(float)P(order,"requiredWork")
        ||!(bool)P(order,"materialsConsumed")||!(bool)P(order,"processFluidConsumed")||!(bool)P(order,"anesthesiaConsumed"))
          throw new InvalidOperationException("First actual surgery clinical/material completion incomplete.");
      var partType=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");
      var parts=((IEnumerable)partType.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(R(partType),null)).Cast<object>().ToArray();
      var first=parts.Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:1");
      var second=parts.Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:2");
      if(!(bool)P(first,"installed")||(string)P(first,"installedSubjectId")!="character:staff:1:01"
        ||(string)P(first,"installationOrderId")!="surgery:1"||string.IsNullOrEmpty((string)P(first,"installationCommitId"))
        ||(bool)P(second,"installed"))throw new InvalidOperationException("Installed first/reserve second part ownership mismatch.");
      var anatomyType=T("IAnatomyHealthRuntime");
      var body=anatomyType.GetMethod("GetAnatomySnapshot",new[]{typeof(string)}).Invoke(R(anatomyType),new object[]{"character:staff:1:01"});
      var arm=((IEnumerable)P(body,"Nodes")).Cast<object>().Single(n=>(string)P(n,"nodeId")=="arm:left");
      if((string)P(arm,"installedPartId")!="surgical-part:1")throw new InvalidOperationException("Body not joined to first part.");
      var itemType=T("IWorldItemStackRuntime");
      var stacks=((IEnumerable)itemType.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(R(itemType),null)).Cast<object>().ToArray();
      string physical=(string)P(first,"physicalItemInstanceId");
      if(stacks.Any(s=>(string)P(s,"ItemInstanceId")==physical)
        ||stacks.Count(s=>(string)P(s,"ItemInstanceId")==(string)P(second,"physicalItemInstanceId")&&(int)P(s,"Quantity")==1)!=1)
          throw new InvalidOperationException("Installed part still physical or reserve part lost/duplicated.");
      SessionState.SetString("wim023-first-installed-physical",physical);
      SessionState.SetString("wim023-second-physical",(string)P(second,"physicalItemInstanceId"));
      result.Log("WIM023 FIRST_ACTUAL_INSTALL_PASS order=surgery:1;part=surgical-part:1;physical={0};node=arm:left;subject=character:staff:1:01;work44;materialProcessAnesthesiaConsumed=true;physicalStack0;reservePart2Stack1;installationCommit={1}",physical,P(first,"installationCommitId"));
    }
}
```

### Preparation plus rejected request
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }


    static object[] Rows(object value) => ((IEnumerable)value).Cast<object>().ToArray();
    static string Id(object part) => (string)P(part,"partInstanceId");
    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale!=0f)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected paused medical setup required.");
        var facility=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("BuildableObject")).OfType<Component>()
            .Single(f=>f.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&f.gameObject.activeInHierarchy&&P(f,"BuildingData")!=null
                &&Convert.ToInt32(P(P(f,"BuildingData"),"id"))==9506
                &&new Vector2Int(50,1).Equals(P(f,"centerPos")));
        var worker=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>()
            .Single(a=>a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&(string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")=="owner");
        object recipe=Resources.LoadAll("SO/Economy/Recipes",T("ProductionRecipeSO"))
            .Single(r=>(string)P(r,"RecipeId")=="recipe:surgery:prosthetic-arm");
        var queryType=T("IProductionBillQuery");var query=R(queryType);
        var ordersType=T("IProductionBillOrderCommand");var orders=R(ordersType);
        var workType=T("IProductionBillWorkExecution");var work=R(workType);
        var partsType=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");var parts=R(partsType);
        var stackType=T("IWorldItemStackRuntime");var stacks=R(stackType);
        var getBills=queryType.GetMethod("GetBills");
        if(Rows(getBills.Invoke(query,new object[]{facility})).Any(b=>(string)P(b,"RecipeId")=="recipe:surgery:prosthetic-arm"))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Preparation bill already exists; inspect instead of replay.");
        var capturedBefore=Rows(partsType.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null));
        if(capturedBefore.Any(p=>(string)P(p,"itemDefinitionId")=="surgery:prosthetic:arm:left"
            && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace((string)P(p,"sourceProductionCommitId"))))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Production-backed preparation part already exists; inspect instead of replay.");
        var before=new HashSet<string>(capturedBefore.Select(Id),StringComparer.Ordinal);
        object added=ordersType.GetMethod("AddBill").Invoke(orders,new object[]{facility,"recipe:surgery:prosthetic-arm",
            Enum.Parse(T("ProductionOrderMode","DungeonStory.Production"),"RepeatCount"),2});
        if(!(bool)P(added,"Succeeded"))throw new InvalidOperationException("M06 AddBill rejected:"+P(added,"Failure"));
        object billId=P(added,"BillId");
        result.Log("WIM023 PREP_BILL id={0};cycles=2;gameplayProductionProof=false",P(billId,"Value"));
        var spawn=stackType.GetMethods().Single(m=>m.Name=="SpawnItemAt"&&m.GetParameters().Length==6);
        object bufferState=Enum.Parse(spawn.GetParameters()[3].ParameterType,"FacilityBuffer");
        for(int cycle=0;cycle<2;cycle++)
        {
            object bill=Rows(getBills.Invoke(query,new object[]{facility})).Single(b=>P(b,"BillId").Equals(billId));
            foreach(object input in Rows(P(bill,"Inputs")))
            {
                int count=(int)P(input,"Amount");
                object[] args={P(input,"ItemId"),count,P(facility,"centerPos"),bufferState,P(bill,"MaterialDestinationId"),0};
                if(!(bool)spawn.Invoke(stacks,args)||(int)args[5]!=count)
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("Exact M06 fixture input rejected:"+P(input,"ItemId"));
                result.Log("WIM023 PREP_INPUT cycle={0};item={1};quantity={2};destination={3}",cycle,P(input,"ItemId"),count,P(bill,"MaterialDestinationId"));
            }
            object available=workType.GetMethod("CheckWorkAvailability").Invoke(work,new object[]{facility,P(recipe,"WorkTypeId")});
            if(!(bool)P(available,"Available"))throw new InvalidOperationException("M06 unavailable:"+P(available,"Failure"));
            object begun=workType.GetMethod("BeginWork").Invoke(work,new object[]{worker,facility,P(recipe,"WorkTypeId")});
            if(!(bool)P(begun,"Succeeded"))throw new InvalidOperationException("M06 BeginWork rejected:"+P(begun,"Failure"));
            object completed=workType.GetMethod("ExecuteWork").Invoke(work,new object[]{worker,facility,billId,(float)P(recipe,"RequiredWork")+1f});
            result.Log("WIM023 PREP_CYCLE cycle={0};succeeded={1};completed={2};outcome={3};failure={4}",cycle,P(completed,"Succeeded"),P(completed,"CycleCompleted"),P(completed,"Outcome"),P(completed,"Failure"));
            if(!(bool)P(completed,"Succeeded")||!(bool)P(completed,"CycleCompleted"))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("M06 cycle incomplete; inspect saved WIP, do not replay.");
        }
        var made=Rows(partsType.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null)).Where(p=>!before.Contains(Id(p))).ToArray();
        var physical=Rows(stackType.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(stacks,null));
        if(made.Length!=2 || made.Select(Id).Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count()!=2
            ||made.Select(p=>(string)P(p,"physicalItemInstanceId")).Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count()!=2
            ||made.Select(p=>(string)P(p,"sourceProductionCommitId")).Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count()!=2)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Two separate production-backed part identities required.");
        foreach(var p in made)
        {
            string item=(string)P(p,"itemDefinitionId"),instance=(string)P(p,"physicalItemInstanceId"),stackId=(string)P(p,"worldStackId");
            if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Id(p))||string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(instance)||string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace((string)P(p,"sourceProductionCommitId"))
              ||item!="surgery:prosthetic:arm:left" ||(string)P(p,"nodeId")!="arm:left" ||(bool)P(p,"installed"))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Invalid prepared part:"+Id(p));
            var matches=physical.Where(s=>(string)P(s,"StackId")==stackId&&(string)P(s,"ItemInstanceId")==instance
                &&(string)P(s,"ItemId")==item&&(int)P(s,"Quantity")==1).ToArray();
            if(matches.Length!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Prepared part physical join invalid:"+Id(p));
            result.Log("WIM023 PREP_PART id={0};instance={1};stack={2};commit={3};quality={4};state={5}",Id(p),instance,stackId,P(p,"sourceProductionCommitId"),P(p,"quality"),P(matches[0],"State"));
        }
        var first=made.Single(p=>Id(p)=="surgical-part:1");
        string firstStack=(string)P(first,"worldStackId");
        var beforeReject=physical.Single(s=>(string)P(s,"StackId")==firstStack);
        object[] rejectArgs={firstStack,0,new Vector2Int(44,1),"surgery-materials:surgery:1",0,null};
        if((bool)stackType.GetMethod("TryRequestStackDelivery").Invoke(stacks,rejectArgs)||(int)rejectArgs[4]!=0)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Zero-quantity delivery unexpectedly accepted.");
        var afterReject=Rows(stackType.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(stacks,null)).Single(s=>(string)P(s,"StackId")==firstStack);
        foreach(string field in new[]{"ItemId","ItemInstanceId","Quantity","State","Position","DestinationId","ReservedQuantity","SourceStorageDestinationId"})
            if(!Equals(P(beforeReject,field),P(afterReject,field)))throw new InvalidOperationException("Rejected delivery changed "+field);
        result.Log("WIM023 OUTPUT_REJECT_NO_MUTATION_PASS invalidAmount=0;sameStackInstanceQuantityStatePositionDestinationReservation=true;failure={0}",rejectArgs[5]);
        result.Log("WIM023 TWO_REAL_PARTS_READY exactOutputCount=2;directProductionWork=fixtureOnly;surgeryApplyWorkCalls=0;actualSurgery=NOT_RUN");
    }
}
```

### Actual selected-output observer
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }



    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale!=0f)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected paused surgery required.");
        const string key="wim023-observe-surgery:1";
        if(SessionState.GetBool(key,false))throw new InvalidOperationException("Observer already registered; inspect existing run.");
        var type=T("ISurgeryQuery");object query=R(type);var get=type.GetMethod("TryGetOrder");
        object[] initial={"surgery:1",null};
        if(!(bool)get.Invoke(query,initial)||(string)P(initial[1],"selectedPartInstanceId")!="surgical-part:1"
          ||Convert.ToString(P(initial[1],"state"))=="Completed")throw new InvalidOperationException("Expected first order unavailable.");
        var partType=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");
        var part=((IEnumerable)partType.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(R(partType),null)).Cast<object>()
            .Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")=="surgical-part:1");
        string selectedStack=(string)P(part,"worldStackId"),selectedInstance=(string)P(part,"physicalItemInstanceId");
        var itemType=T("IWorldItemStackRuntime");object items=R(itemType);bool sawRoutable=false;
        double start=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;float gameStart=Time.time;int last=-1;
        SessionState.SetBool(key,true);
        EditorApplication.CallbackFunction tick=null;
        tick=()=>{
          try{
            if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying)throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected Play ended during observation.");
            object[] args={"surgery:1",null};
            if(!(bool)get.Invoke(query,args))throw new InvalidOperationException("Observed order disappeared.");
            object order=args[1];int window=(int)((Time.time-gameStart)/30f);
            bool terminal=!(bool)P(order,"IsActive");
            bool expired=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start>=150d||Time.time-gameStart>=360f;
            if(window!=last||terminal||expired){
              last=window;
              if(!sawRoutable){
                var routed=((IEnumerable)itemType.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(items,null)).Cast<object>()
                    .SingleOrDefault(s=>(string)P(s,"StackId")==selectedStack);
                if(routed!=null&&(string)P(routed,"DestinationId")==(string)P(order,"materialDestinationId")){
                  if(Convert.ToString(P(routed,"State"))=="FacilityOutputBuffer"
                    ||(int)P(routed,"Quantity")!=1||(string)P(routed,"ItemInstanceId")!=selectedInstance)
                      throw new InvalidOperationException("Selected output retarget state/identity/quantity invalid.");
                  sawRoutable=true;
                  Debug.Log("WIM023 SELECTED_OUTPUT_ROUTABLE_PASS stack="+selectedStack+";instance="+selectedInstance+";state="+P(routed,"State")+";quantity=1;position="+P(routed,"Position"));
                }
              }
              Debug.Log("WIM023 AI_WINDOW order=surgery:1;state="+P(order,"state")+";work="+P(order,"completedWork")+"/"+P(order,"requiredWork")
                +";doctor="+P(order,"doctorId")+";patientAdmitted="+P(order,"patientAdmitted")+";materialConsumed="+P(order,"materialsConsumed")
                +";admission="+P(order,"admissionX")+","+P(order,"admissionY")+";status="+P(P(order,"statusData"),"code")+";gameElapsed="+(Time.time-gameStart)+";realElapsed="+(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start));
            }
            if(terminal||expired){
              EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(key,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
              Debug.Log("WIM023 AI_OBSERVER_TERMINAL cause="+(terminal?"ORDER_TERMINAL":"BOUNDED_OBSERVATION_END")+";state="+P(order,"state")+";result="+P(order,"resultSucceeded"));
            }
          }catch(Exception ex){
            EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(key,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
            Debug.LogError("WIM023 AI_OBSERVER_ERROR "+ex);
          }
        };
        EditorApplication.update+=tick;Time.timeScale=8f;EditorApplication.isPaused=false;
        result.Log("WIM023 AI_OBSERVER_STARTED order=surgery:1;maxReal=150;maxGame=360;scale=8;initialGame={0};initialReal={1};actualWorkOnly=true",gameStart,start);
    }
}
```

### Revised first Schedule
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }


    static void Click(UnityEngine.UI.Button button)
    {
        if(!button.IsInteractable() || UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current==null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual UI input unavailable: "+button.name);
        var pointer=new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerEnterHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerDownHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerUpHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerClickHandler);
    }


    static object[] Rows(object o)=>((IEnumerable)o).Cast<object>().ToArray();
    static void SetPriority(object actor, string workId)
    {
        var ability=actor.GetType().GetMethods().Single(m=>m.Name=="GetAbility"&&m.IsGenericMethodDefinition)
            .MakeGenericMethod(T("AbilityWork")).Invoke(actor,null);
        var priorities=P(ability,"WorkPriorities");var setter=priorities.GetType().GetMethod("SetPriority");
        object id=Activator.CreateInstance(T("WorkTypeId","DungeonStory.Work"),new object[]{workId});
        setter.Invoke(priorities,new object[]{id,Enum.Parse(setter.GetParameters()[1].ParameterType,"Priority1")});
        var duty=ability.GetType().GetMethod("SetDutyState");
        duty.Invoke(ability,new object[]{Enum.Parse(duty.GetParameters()[0].ParameterType,"OnDuty")});
    }
    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale!=0f)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected paused setup required.");
        var surgeryType=T("ISurgeryQuery");object surgery=R(surgeryType);
        if(Rows(surgeryType.GetProperty("ActiveOrders").GetValue(surgery)).Length!=0)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Existing active surgery; inspect, do not replay Schedule.");
        var partsType=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");object parts=R(partsType);
        var prepared=Rows(partsType.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null));
        if(prepared.Count(p=>(string)P(p,"itemDefinitionId")=="surgery:prosthetic:arm:left"&&!(bool)P(p,"installed"))!=2)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Exactly two prepared uninstalled arms required before first surgery.");
        var root=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterSurgeryWindowView")).OfType<Component>()
            .Single(c=>c.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&c.gameObject.activeInHierarchy).transform;
        var tmp=T("TMPro.TMP_Text","Unity.TextMeshPro");
        Func<string,string> label=row=>(string)P(root.Find("SurgeryPanel/"+row+"/Value").GetComponent(tmp),"text");
        if(label("ProcedureRow")!="보철 설치"||label("TargetRow")!="왼팔")
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Previously verified actual selection changed.");
        Click(root.Find("SurgeryPanel/ProcedureRow/Previous").GetComponent<UnityEngine.UI.Button>());
        Click(root.Find("SurgeryPanel/ProcedureRow/Next").GetComponent<UnityEngine.UI.Button>());
        foreach(var actor in Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>().Where(c=>c.gameObject.scene.isLoaded))
        {
            string id=(string)P(P(actor,"Identity"),"PersistentId");
            if(id=="owner")SetPriority(actor,"work:surgery");
            if(id=="character:staff:1:02")SetPriority(actor,"work:haul");
        }
        var randomType=T("DungeonStory.Foundation.IRandomStreamProvider","DungeonStory.Foundation");
        object random=R(randomType),stream=randomType.GetMethod("Get").Invoke(random,new object[]{"medical:surgery-outcomes"});
        var streamType=T("DungeonStory.Foundation.IRandomStream","DungeonStory.Foundation");
        result.Log("WIM023 CONTROLLED_INITIAL_SURGERY_RNG before={0};new=1;onlyBeforeFirstSchedule=true",streamType.GetProperty("State").GetValue(stream));
        streamType.GetMethod("Restore").Invoke(stream,new object[]{1UL});
        Click(root.Find("SurgeryPanel/Commands/Schedule").GetComponent<UnityEngine.UI.Button>());
        result.Log("WIM023 ACTUAL_SCHEDULE_UI details={0}",P(root.Find("SurgeryPanel/Details").GetComponent(tmp),"text"));
        var active=Rows(surgeryType.GetProperty("ActiveOrders").GetValue(surgery));
        if(active.Length!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual UI did not schedule exactly one order.");
        object order=active[0];
        if((string)P(order,"procedureId")!="procedure:prosthetic-installation"||(string)P(order,"targetNodeId")!="arm:left"
          ||(string)P(P(order,"subject"),"subjectId")!="character:staff:1:01"||(string)P(order,"preferredDoctorId")!="owner")
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual UI scheduled wrong subject/doctor/procedure.");
        var expectedMaterials=new Dictionary<string,int>(StringComparer.Ordinal)
        {
            {"material:lumber",2},{"medicine:anesthetic",1},
            {"medicine:disinfectant",1},{"resource:clean-water",1}
        };
        var mandatory=Rows(P(order,"materials")).Where(r=>!(bool)P(r,"optional")).ToArray();
        if(mandatory.Length!=expectedMaterials.Count
          ||mandatory.Any(r=>!expectedMaterials.TryGetValue((string)P(r,"itemId"),out int expected)
            ||(int)P(r,"quantity")!=expected)
          ||mandatory.Select(r=>(string)P(r,"itemId")).Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count()!=expectedMaterials.Count)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Authored surgical BOM must be lumber2/anesthetic1/disinfectant1/water1; no phantom +1.");
        var table=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("BuildableObject")).OfType<Component>()
            .Single(f=>f.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&Convert.ToString(P(f,"PersistentInstanceId"))==(string)P(order,"facilityId"));
        var grid=P(table,"Grid");
        var access=table.GetType().GetMethod("IsWorkAccessGridPosition");
        if(!(bool)access.Invoke(table,new object[]{grid,new Vector2Int(42,1)})
          ||(bool)access.Invoke(table,new object[]{grid,new Vector2Int(43,0)}))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Same-floor legal42,1 / wrong-floor43,0 access contract failed.");
        object[] nearest={grid,new Vector2Int(19,0),null};
        if(!(bool)table.GetType().GetMethod("TryGetNearestWorkAccessGridPosition").Invoke(table,nearest)
          ||!new Vector2Int(42,1).Equals(nearest[2]))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Expected legal admission42,1 from original19,0.");
        result.Log("WIM023 AUTHORED_BOM_AND_ACCESS_PASS lumber2/anesthetic1/disinfectant1/water1;processWaterSeparate=1;legal42,1;wrongFloor43,0Rejected=true");
        string selected=(string)P(order,"selectedPartInstanceId");
        var part=prepared.Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")==selected);
        var stackType=T("IWorldItemStackRuntime");var stacks=R(stackType);
        var spawn=stackType.GetMethods().Single(m=>m.Name=="SpawnItemAt"&&m.GetParameters().Length==6);
        object loose=Enum.Parse(spawn.GetParameters()[3].ParameterType,"Loose");
        foreach(var req in Rows(P(order,"materials")).Where(r=>!(bool)P(r,"optional")))
        {
            int count=(int)P(req,"quantity");object[] args={P(req,"itemId"),count,new Vector2Int(43,1),loose,string.Empty,0};
            if(!(bool)spawn.Invoke(stacks,args)||(int)args[5]!=count)throw new InvalidOperationException("Exact ordinary-source preparation failed:"+P(req,"itemId"));
            result.Log("WIM023 ORDINARY_SOURCE item={0};quantity={1};destination=none;position=43,1;haul=normalAI",P(req,"itemId"),count);
        }
        object[] waterArgs={"resource:clean-water",1,new Vector2Int(43,1),loose,string.Empty,0};
        if(!(bool)spawn.Invoke(stacks,waterArgs)||(int)waterArgs[5]!=1)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Authored M03 manual process-water source unavailable.");
        result.Log("WIM023 FIRST_ORDER_SCHEDULED id={0};part={1};instance={2};quality={3};state={4};work={5}/{6};materialsDestination={7};sourceOnlyProcessWater=1;surgeryWork=actualAIOnly",
            P(order,"orderId"),selected,P(part,"physicalItemInstanceId"),P(part,"quality"),P(order,"state"),P(order,"completedWork"),P(order,"requiredWork"),P(order,"materialDestinationId"));
        result.Log("WIM023 FIRST_ORDER_READY game={0};real={1};stillPaused=true",Time.time,EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup);
    }
}
```

### Revised two-part preparation
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }


    static object[] Rows(object value) => ((IEnumerable)value).Cast<object>().ToArray();
    static string Id(object part) => (string)P(part,"partInstanceId");
    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale!=0f)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected paused medical setup required.");
        var facility=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("BuildableObject")).OfType<Component>()
            .Single(f=>f.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&f.gameObject.activeInHierarchy&&P(f,"BuildingData")!=null
                &&Convert.ToInt32(P(P(f,"BuildingData"),"id"))==9506
                &&new Vector2Int(50,1).Equals(P(f,"centerPos")));
        var worker=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>()
            .Single(a=>a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&(string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")=="owner");
        object recipe=Resources.LoadAll("SO/Economy/Recipes",T("ProductionRecipeSO"))
            .Single(r=>(string)P(r,"RecipeId")=="recipe:surgery:prosthetic-arm");
        var queryType=T("IProductionBillQuery");var query=R(queryType);
        var ordersType=T("IProductionBillOrderCommand");var orders=R(ordersType);
        var workType=T("IProductionBillWorkExecution");var work=R(workType);
        var partsType=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");var parts=R(partsType);
        var stackType=T("IWorldItemStackRuntime");var stacks=R(stackType);
        var getBills=queryType.GetMethod("GetBills");
        if(Rows(getBills.Invoke(query,new object[]{facility})).Any(b=>(string)P(b,"RecipeId")=="recipe:surgery:prosthetic-arm"))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Preparation bill already exists; inspect instead of replay.");
        var capturedBefore=Rows(partsType.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null));
        if(capturedBefore.Any(p=>(string)P(p,"itemDefinitionId")=="surgery:prosthetic:arm:left"
            && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace((string)P(p,"sourceProductionCommitId"))))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Production-backed preparation part already exists; inspect instead of replay.");
        var before=new HashSet<string>(capturedBefore.Select(Id),StringComparer.Ordinal);
        object added=ordersType.GetMethod("AddBill").Invoke(orders,new object[]{facility,"recipe:surgery:prosthetic-arm",
            Enum.Parse(T("ProductionOrderMode","DungeonStory.Production"),"RepeatCount"),2});
        if(!(bool)P(added,"Succeeded"))throw new InvalidOperationException("M06 AddBill rejected:"+P(added,"Failure"));
        object billId=P(added,"BillId");
        result.Log("WIM023 PREP_BILL id={0};cycles=2;gameplayProductionProof=false",P(billId,"Value"));
        var spawn=stackType.GetMethods().Single(m=>m.Name=="SpawnItemAt"&&m.GetParameters().Length==6);
        object bufferState=Enum.Parse(spawn.GetParameters()[3].ParameterType,"FacilityBuffer");
        for(int cycle=0;cycle<2;cycle++)
        {
            object bill=Rows(getBills.Invoke(query,new object[]{facility})).Single(b=>P(b,"BillId").Equals(billId));
            foreach(object input in Rows(P(bill,"Inputs")))
            {
                int count=(int)P(input,"Amount");
                object[] args={P(input,"ItemId"),count,P(facility,"centerPos"),bufferState,P(bill,"MaterialDestinationId"),0};
                if(!(bool)spawn.Invoke(stacks,args)||(int)args[5]!=count)
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("Exact M06 fixture input rejected:"+P(input,"ItemId"));
                result.Log("WIM023 PREP_INPUT cycle={0};item={1};quantity={2};destination={3}",cycle,P(input,"ItemId"),count,P(bill,"MaterialDestinationId"));
            }
            object available=workType.GetMethod("CheckWorkAvailability").Invoke(work,new object[]{facility,P(recipe,"WorkTypeId")});
            if(!(bool)P(available,"Available"))throw new InvalidOperationException("M06 unavailable:"+P(available,"Failure"));
            object begun=workType.GetMethod("BeginWork").Invoke(work,new object[]{worker,facility,P(recipe,"WorkTypeId")});
            if(!(bool)P(begun,"Succeeded"))throw new InvalidOperationException("M06 BeginWork rejected:"+P(begun,"Failure"));
            object completed=workType.GetMethod("ExecuteWork").Invoke(work,new object[]{worker,facility,billId,(float)P(recipe,"RequiredWork")+1f});
            result.Log("WIM023 PREP_CYCLE cycle={0};succeeded={1};completed={2};outcome={3};failure={4}",cycle,P(completed,"Succeeded"),P(completed,"CycleCompleted"),P(completed,"Outcome"),P(completed,"Failure"));
            if(!(bool)P(completed,"Succeeded")||!(bool)P(completed,"CycleCompleted"))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("M06 cycle incomplete; inspect saved WIP, do not replay.");
        }
        var made=Rows(partsType.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null)).Where(p=>!before.Contains(Id(p))).ToArray();
        var physical=Rows(stackType.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(stacks,null));
        if(made.Length!=2 || made.Select(Id).Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count()!=2
            ||made.Select(p=>(string)P(p,"physicalItemInstanceId")).Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count()!=2
            ||made.Select(p=>(string)P(p,"sourceProductionCommitId")).Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count()!=2)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Two separate production-backed part identities required.");
        foreach(var p in made)
        {
            string item=(string)P(p,"itemDefinitionId"),instance=(string)P(p,"physicalItemInstanceId"),stackId=(string)P(p,"worldStackId");
            if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Id(p))||string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(instance)||string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace((string)P(p,"sourceProductionCommitId"))
              ||item!="surgery:prosthetic:arm:left" ||(string)P(p,"nodeId")!="arm:left" ||(bool)P(p,"installed"))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Invalid prepared part:"+Id(p));
            var matches=physical.Where(s=>(string)P(s,"StackId")==stackId&&(string)P(s,"ItemInstanceId")==instance
                &&(string)P(s,"ItemId")==item&&(int)P(s,"Quantity")==1).ToArray();
            if(matches.Length!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Prepared part physical join invalid:"+Id(p));
            result.Log("WIM023 PREP_PART id={0};instance={1};stack={2};commit={3};quality={4};state={5}",Id(p),instance,stackId,P(p,"sourceProductionCommitId"),P(p,"quality"),P(matches[0],"State"));
        }
        result.Log("WIM023 TWO_REAL_PARTS_READY exactOutputCount=2;directProductionWork=fixtureOnly;surgeryApplyWorkCalls=0;actualSurgery=NOT_RUN");
    }
}
```

### Revised bounded actual AI observer
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }



    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale!=0f)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected paused surgery required.");
        const string key="wim023-observe-surgery:1";
        if(SessionState.GetBool(key,false))throw new InvalidOperationException("Observer already registered; inspect existing run.");
        var type=T("ISurgeryQuery");object query=R(type);var get=type.GetMethod("TryGetOrder");
        object[] initial={"surgery:1",null};
        if(!(bool)get.Invoke(query,initial)||(string)P(initial[1],"selectedPartInstanceId")!="surgical-part:1"
          ||Convert.ToString(P(initial[1],"state"))=="Completed")throw new InvalidOperationException("Expected first order unavailable.");
        double start=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;float gameStart=Time.time;int last=-1;
        SessionState.SetBool(key,true);
        EditorApplication.CallbackFunction tick=null;
        tick=()=>{
          try{
            if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying)throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected Play ended during observation.");
            object[] args={"surgery:1",null};
            if(!(bool)get.Invoke(query,args))throw new InvalidOperationException("Observed order disappeared.");
            object order=args[1];int window=(int)((Time.time-gameStart)/30f);
            bool terminal=!(bool)P(order,"IsActive");
            bool expired=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start>=150d||Time.time-gameStart>=360f;
            if(window!=last||terminal||expired){
              last=window;
              Debug.Log("WIM023 AI_WINDOW order=surgery:1;state="+P(order,"state")+";work="+P(order,"completedWork")+"/"+P(order,"requiredWork")
                +";doctor="+P(order,"doctorId")+";patientAdmitted="+P(order,"patientAdmitted")+";materialConsumed="+P(order,"materialsConsumed")
                +";admission="+P(order,"admissionX")+","+P(order,"admissionY")+";status="+P(P(order,"statusData"),"code")+";gameElapsed="+(Time.time-gameStart)+";realElapsed="+(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start));
            }
            if(terminal||expired){
              EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(key,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
              Debug.Log("WIM023 AI_OBSERVER_TERMINAL cause="+(terminal?"ORDER_TERMINAL":"BOUNDED_OBSERVATION_END")+";state="+P(order,"state")+";result="+P(order,"resultSucceeded"));
            }
          }catch(Exception ex){
            EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(key,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
            Debug.LogError("WIM023 AI_OBSERVER_ERROR "+ex);
          }
        };
        EditorApplication.update+=tick;Time.timeScale=8f;EditorApplication.isPaused=false;
        result.Log("WIM023 AI_OBSERVER_STARTED order=surgery:1;maxReal=150;maxGame=360;scale=8;initialGame={0};initialReal={1};actualWorkOnly=true",gameStart,start);
    }
}
```

## 2026-09-10 first actual UI/AI attempt: terminal diagnosis

First actual UI Schedule succeeded. Actual preparation/order source was independently reviewed once; no direct surgery work or changed risk chance. Observation reached real150.5302872s/game327.027s, PatientWaiting0/44, status PatientCurrentMovePending, no clinical outcome rolled. Observer removed itself and paused; confirmed no active observer before protected Stop219190.1345653/allfalse. All5protected hashes exact.

Confirmed source defects: (1) SurgicalMaterialRequirement.quantity defaults1; Add adds authored quantity without initializing0, so actual order3/2/2/2 is a common +1 error, not authoring. (2) nearest admission43,0 is walkable but no geometric route from19,0; legal alternatives42,1 and46,1 have24/28steps. Pure Grid.SearchPathTo diagnostic used no actor filter, so this proves geometric wrong-candidate selection, not full actor permission approval. Actual source correction is pending; no path broker/door/actor movement was manually changed to pass. Closed WIM count remains30/47; this partial actual Schedule is not whole surgery/AI PASS.

### Bounded observer executed source
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }



    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale!=0f)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected paused surgery required.");
        const string key="wim023-observe-surgery:1";
        if(SessionState.GetBool(key,false))throw new InvalidOperationException("Observer already registered; inspect existing run.");
        var type=T("ISurgeryQuery");object query=R(type);var get=type.GetMethod("TryGetOrder");
        object[] initial={"surgery:1",null};
        if(!(bool)get.Invoke(query,initial)||(string)P(initial[1],"selectedPartInstanceId")!="surgical-part:1"
          ||Convert.ToString(P(initial[1],"state"))=="Completed")throw new InvalidOperationException("Expected first order unavailable.");
        double start=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;float gameStart=Time.time;int last=-1;
        SessionState.SetBool(key,true);
        EditorApplication.CallbackFunction tick=null;
        tick=()=>{
          try{
            if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying)throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected Play ended during observation.");
            object[] args={"surgery:1",null};
            if(!(bool)get.Invoke(query,args))throw new InvalidOperationException("Observed order disappeared.");
            object order=args[1];int window=(int)((Time.time-gameStart)/30f);
            bool terminal=!(bool)P(order,"IsActive");
            bool expired=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start>=150d||Time.time-gameStart>=360f;
            if(window!=last||terminal||expired){
              last=window;
              Debug.Log("WIM023 AI_WINDOW order=surgery:1;state="+P(order,"state")+";work="+P(order,"completedWork")+"/"+P(order,"requiredWork")
                +";doctor="+P(order,"doctorId")+";patientAdmitted="+P(order,"patientAdmitted")+";materialConsumed="+P(order,"materialsConsumed")
                +";status="+P(P(order,"statusData"),"code")+";gameElapsed="+(Time.time-gameStart)+";realElapsed="+(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start));
            }
            if(terminal||expired){
              EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(key,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
              Debug.Log("WIM023 AI_OBSERVER_TERMINAL cause="+(terminal?"ORDER_TERMINAL":"BOUNDED_OBSERVATION_END")+";state="+P(order,"state")+";result="+P(order,"resultSucceeded"));
            }
          }catch(Exception ex){
            EditorApplication.update-=tick;SessionState.SetBool(key,false);Time.timeScale=0f;EditorApplication.isPaused=true;
            Debug.LogError("WIM023 AI_OBSERVER_ERROR "+ex);
          }
        };
        EditorApplication.update+=tick;Time.timeScale=8f;EditorApplication.isPaused=false;
        result.Log("WIM023 AI_OBSERVER_STARTED order=surgery:1;maxReal=150;maxGame=360;scale=8;initialGame={0};initialReal={1};actualWorkOnly=true",gameStart,start);
    }
}
```

### Research-gated space prerequisite

Scope: declared preparation unless explicitly the actual EventSystem selector/window input; NOT actual doctor-AI surgery completion.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name) => value.GetType().GetProperty(name).GetValue(value);
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }
    static void Visit(object project, HashSet<string> visiting, HashSet<string> visited, List<object> order)
    {
        string id = Convert.ToString(P(project, "ProjectId"));
        if (visited.Contains(id)) return;
        if (!visiting.Add(id)) throw new InvalidOperationException("Authored research cycle: " + id);
        foreach (object prerequisite in ((IEnumerable)P(project, "Prerequisites")).Cast<object>()
            .OrderBy(v => Convert.ToString(P(v, "ProjectId")), StringComparer.Ordinal))
            Visit(prerequisite, visiting, visited, order);
        visiting.Remove(id); visited.Add(id); order.Add(project);
    }
    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if (!EditorApplication.isPlaying || Time.timeScale != 0f)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Requires protected frozen-time fixture.");
        object target=Resources.LoadAll("SO/Research/Projects",T("ResearchProjectSO","DungeonStory.Research"))
            .Single(p=>Convert.ToString(P(p,"ProjectId"))=="research:mining:quarry");
        var order=new List<object>();
        Visit(target,new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal),new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal),order);
        object research=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("BlueprintResearchRuntime")).OfType<Component>().Single(c=>c.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object projects=P(P(research,"State"),"Projects");
        foreach(object project in order) {
            object id=P(project,"ProjectId");
            if((bool)projects.GetType().GetMethod("IsCompleted").Invoke(projects,new[]{id}))continue;
            object[] args={id,null};
            if(!(bool)research.GetType().GetMethod("TryCompleteProjectImmediatelyForVerification").Invoke(research,args))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Fixture research: "+id+":"+args[1]);
        }
        object expansion=R(T("IDungeonSpaceExpansionQuery"));
        object last=P(expansion,"LastResult");
        if(Convert.ToInt32(P(last,"CurrentInteriorColumns"))!=51)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Normal research event did not expand to authored Tier1.");
        result.Log("WIM023 FIXTURE_SPACE_READY research=research:mining:quarry;before={0};after={1};tier={2};setupOnly=true;noDebugExpansion=true",
            P(last,"PreviousInteriorColumns"),P(last,"CurrentInteriorColumns"),P(last,"Tier"));
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System; using System.Collections; using System.Linq; using UnityEngine; using UnityEditor;
internal class CommandScript:IRunCommand {
 static Type T(string n)=>Type.GetType(n+", Assembly-CSharp",true);
 static object P(object o,string n) { var t=o.GetType(); var p=t.GetProperty(n); if(p!=null)return p.GetValue(o); var f=t.GetField(n); if(f!=null)return f.GetValue(o); throw new InvalidOperationException("Public member unavailable: "+t.FullName+"."+n); }
 public void Execute(ExecutionResult result) {
 if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying || Time.timeScale!=0f)throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected paused fixture required.");
 var scope=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope")).OfType<Component>().Single(c=>c.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
 var container=P(scope,"Container");var resolve=container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve",new[]{typeof(Type),typeof(object)});
 var gm=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("GridSystemManager")).OfType<Component>().Single(c=>c.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
 var grid=P(gm,"grid");
 var infoType=T("InitialBuildInfo");var definitionType=infoType.GetField("Building").FieldType;
 var all=Resources.LoadAll("SO/Building",definitionType);
 Func<int,object> def=id=>all.Single(d=>Convert.ToInt32(P(d,"id"))==id);
 var hall=def(0);var door=def(1);var wall=def(7);
 var layer=P(wall,"layer");
 Func<Vector2Int,object> cellAt=p=>grid.GetType().GetMethod("GetGridCell",new[]{typeof(Vector2Int)}).Invoke(grid,new object[]{p});
 var boundary=cellAt(new Vector2Int(42,1));
 var existing=boundary.GetType().GetMethod("GetOccupant").Invoke(boundary,new[]{layer});
 if(existing==null || Convert.ToInt32(P(P(existing,"BuildingData"),"id"))!=7)
   throw new InvalidOperationException("Fixture boundary is not the exact observed empty structural wall42,1.");
 for(int x=43;x<=56;x++) {
  var cell=cellAt(new Vector2Int(x,1));
  if(cell==null || Convert.ToString(P(cell,"AreaType"))!="DungeonInterior"
    || !(bool)cell.GetType().GetMethod("CanOccupy").Invoke(cell,new[]{layer}))
    throw new InvalidOperationException("Fixture room would overlap existing content at "+x+",1.");
 }
 var controller=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonStoryGridBuildingController")).OfType<Component>().Single(c=>c.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
 object[] destroyArgs={existing,null};
 bool removed=(bool)controller.GetType().GetMethod("TryDestroyBuilding").Invoke(controller,destroyArgs);
 if(!removed || boundary.GetType().GetMethod("GetOccupant").Invoke(boundary,new[]{layer})!=null) {
  result.Log("WIM023 FIXTURE_ROOM_NOT_READY boundaryRemovalAccepted={0};reason={1};noPlacementStarted=true",removed,destroyArgs[1]);return;
 }
 var entries=new System.Collections.Generic.List<object>();
 Action<object,Vector2Int> add=(definition,position)=>{var info=Activator.CreateInstance(infoType);infoType.GetField("Building").SetValue(info,definition);infoType.GetField("Position").SetValue(info,position);entries.Add(info);};
 for(int x=42;x<=56;x++)add(hall,new Vector2Int(x,1));
 add(door,new Vector2Int(42,1));add(wall,new Vector2Int(56,1));
 add(def(9503),new Vector2Int(44,1));add(def(9504),new Vector2Int(47,1));add(def(9505),new Vector2Int(48,1));add(def(9506),new Vector2Int(50,1));
 var typed=Array.CreateInstance(infoType,entries.Count);for(int i=0;i<entries.Count;i++)typed.SetValue(entries[i],i);
 object[] placeArgs={typed,null};
 if(!(bool)controller.GetType().GetMethod("TryPlaceInitialBuildings").Invoke(controller,placeArgs))
   throw new InvalidOperationException("Declared initial-placement fixture failed: "+placeArgs[1]);
 object primary=null;
 foreach(var pair in new[]{new[]{9503,44},new[]{9504,47},new[]{9505,48},new[]{9506,50}}) {
  var cell=cellAt(new Vector2Int(pair[1],1));var placed=cell.GetType().GetMethod("GetOccupant").Invoke(cell,new[]{layer});
  if(placed==null || Convert.ToInt32(P(P(placed,"BuildingData"),"id"))!=pair[0])
   throw new InvalidOperationException("Fixture medical placement missing:"+pair[0]);
  if(pair[0]==9503)primary=placed;
  result.Log("WIM023 FIXTURE_FACILITY id={0};position={1};persistentId={2}",pair[0],new Vector2Int(pair[1],1),P(placed,"PersistentInstanceId"));
 }
 var queryType=T("ISurgicalFacilityQuery");var query=resolve.Invoke(container,new object[]{queryType,null});
 var method=queryType.GetMethod("Evaluate");var tags=Enum.ToObject(method.GetParameters()[1].ParameterType,28);
 var assessment=method.Invoke(query,new[]{primary,tags});
 if(!Convert.ToBoolean(P(assessment,"IsAvailable")))throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual surgical facility unavailable:"+P(assessment,"BlockFailure"));
 result.Log("WIM023 FIXTURE_ROOM_FACILITIES_READY sameRoomSupport=true;requiredTags=28;primary=44,1;M06=50,1;existingWallReplacedViaCommand=true;noActorTeleport=true;setupOnly=true;actualSurgery=NOT_RUN");
 }
}
```

### Actual health/surgery window open

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name) => value.GetType().GetProperty(name).GetValue(value);
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }

    static UnityEngine.UI.Button Button(string name) =>
        Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll<UnityEngine.UI.Button>().Single(b=>b.gameObject.scene.isLoaded
            && b.gameObject.activeInHierarchy && b.name==name);
    static void Click(UnityEngine.UI.Button button)
    {
        if(!button.IsInteractable() || UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current==null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual UI input unavailable: "+button.name);
        var pointer=new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerEnterHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerDownHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerUpHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerClickHandler);
    }
    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying || Time.timeScale!=0f)throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected fixture required.");
        var actorType=T("CharacterActor");
        object patient=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(actorType).OfType<Component>().Single(a=>a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&a.gameObject.activeInHierarchy
            && (string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")=="character:staff:1:01");
        var show=T("GameEventBusInfoFeedExtensions").GetMethod("ShowInfo");
        var bus=R(show.GetParameters()[0].ParameterType);
        show.Invoke(null,new[]{bus,patient});
        Click(Button("HealthTab"));Click(Button("SurgeryCommand"));
        var root=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterSurgeryWindowView")).OfType<Component>()
            .Single(c=>c.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&c.gameObject.activeInHierarchy).transform;
        var textType=Type.GetType("TMPro.TMP_Text, Unity.TextMeshPro",true);
        foreach(var row in new[]{"ProcedureRow","TargetRow","PartRow","DoctorRow","FacilityRow"}) {
            var value=root.Find("SurgeryPanel/"+row+"/Value");
            if(value==null)throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual surgery selector missing: "+row);
            result.Log("WIM023 SURGERY_UI row={0};text={1}",row,P(value.GetComponent(textType),"text"));
        }
        result.Log("WIM023 HEALTH_SURGERY_UI_OPEN patient=character:staff:1:01;selectionViaProductionEvent=true;healthAndSurgeryActualPointerClicks=true;scheduled=false");
    }
}
```

```csharp
            && b.gameObject.activeInHierarchy && b.name==name);

    {
        if(!button.IsInteractable() || UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current==null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual UI input unavailable: "+button.name);
        var pointer=new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerEnterHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerDownHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerUpHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerClickHandler);
    }

    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||Time.timeScale!=0f)throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected paused fixture required.");
        var root=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterSurgeryWindowView")).OfType<Component>()
            .Single(c=>c.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&c.gameObject.activeInHierarchy).transform;
        var textType=Type.GetType("TMPro.TMP_Text, Unity.TextMeshPro",true);
        Func<string,string> label=row=>(string)P(root.Find("SurgeryPanel/"+row+"/Value").GetComponent(textType),"text");
        Action<string,string,int> select=(row,expected,limit)=>{
            for(int i=0;i<limit && label(row)!=expected;i++)Click(root.Find("SurgeryPanel/"+row+"/Next").GetComponent<UnityEngine.UI.Button>());
            if(label(row)!=expected)throw new InvalidOperationException("Authored surgery UI option unavailable:"+row+":"+expected);
        };
        object procedure=Resources.LoadAll("SO/Medical/Procedures",T("SurgicalProcedureSO","DungeonStory.Medical"))
            .Single(p=>(string)P(p,"ProcedureId")=="procedure:prosthetic-installation");
        object catalog=R(T("IAnatomyProfileCatalog","DungeonStory.Medical"));
        object profile=catalog.GetType().GetMethod("GetForSpecies").Invoke(catalog,new object[]{"Orc"});
        object[] args={"arm:left",null};
        if(!(bool)profile.GetType().GetMethod("TryGetNode").Invoke(profile,args))throw new InvalidOperationException("Authored arm missing.");
        select("ProcedureRow",(string)P(procedure,"DisplayName"),Resources.LoadAll("SO/Medical/Procedures",T("SurgicalProcedureSO","DungeonStory.Medical")).Count(p=>(bool)P(p,"AllowsLivingSubject")));
        select("TargetRow",(string)P(args[1],"DisplayName"),64);
        foreach(string row in new[]{"ProcedureRow","TargetRow","PartRow","DoctorRow","FacilityRow"})
            result.Log("WIM023 SELECTED row={0};text={1}",row,label(row));
        result.Log("WIM023 PROSTHETIC_UI_SELECTION_READY procedure=procedure:prosthetic-installation;target=arm:left;actualPointerSelectors=true;scheduled=false;physicalParts=NOT_READY");
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }


    static void Click(UnityEngine.UI.Button button)
    {
        if(!button.IsInteractable() || UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current==null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual UI input unavailable: "+button.name);
        var pointer=new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerEnterHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerDownHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerUpHandler);
        UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.Execute(button.gameObject,pointer,UnityEngine.EventSystems.ExecuteEvents.pointerClickHandler);
    }


    static object[] Rows(object o)=>((IEnumerable)o).Cast<object>().ToArray();
    static void SetPriority(object actor, string workId)
    {
        var ability=actor.GetType().GetMethods().Single(m=>m.Name=="GetAbility"&&m.IsGenericMethodDefinition)
            .MakeGenericMethod(T("AbilityWork")).Invoke(actor,null);
        var priorities=P(ability,"WorkPriorities");var setter=priorities.GetType().GetMethod("SetPriority");
        object id=Activator.CreateInstance(T("WorkTypeId","DungeonStory.Work"),new object[]{workId});
        setter.Invoke(priorities,new object[]{id,Enum.Parse(setter.GetParameters()[1].ParameterType,"Priority1")});
        var duty=ability.GetType().GetMethod("SetDutyState");
        duty.Invoke(ability,new object[]{Enum.Parse(duty.GetParameters()[0].ParameterType,"OnDuty")});
    }
    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale!=0f)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected paused setup required.");
        var surgeryType=T("ISurgeryQuery");object surgery=R(surgeryType);
        if(Rows(surgeryType.GetProperty("ActiveOrders").GetValue(surgery)).Length!=0)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Existing active surgery; inspect, do not replay Schedule.");
        var partsType=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");object parts=R(partsType);
        var prepared=Rows(partsType.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null));
        if(prepared.Count(p=>(string)P(p,"itemDefinitionId")=="surgery:prosthetic:arm:left"&&!(bool)P(p,"installed"))!=2)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Exactly two prepared uninstalled arms required before first surgery.");
        var root=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterSurgeryWindowView")).OfType<Component>()
            .Single(c=>c.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&c.gameObject.activeInHierarchy).transform;
        var tmp=T("TMPro.TMP_Text","Unity.TextMeshPro");
        Func<string,string> label=row=>(string)P(root.Find("SurgeryPanel/"+row+"/Value").GetComponent(tmp),"text");
        if(label("ProcedureRow")!="보철 설치"||label("TargetRow")!="왼팔")
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Previously verified actual selection changed.");
        Click(root.Find("SurgeryPanel/ProcedureRow/Previous").GetComponent<UnityEngine.UI.Button>());
        Click(root.Find("SurgeryPanel/ProcedureRow/Next").GetComponent<UnityEngine.UI.Button>());
        foreach(var actor in Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>().Where(c=>c.gameObject.scene.isLoaded))
        {
            string id=(string)P(P(actor,"Identity"),"PersistentId");
            if(id=="owner")SetPriority(actor,"work:surgery");
            if(id=="character:staff:1:02")SetPriority(actor,"work:haul");
        }
        var randomType=T("DungeonStory.Foundation.IRandomStreamProvider","DungeonStory.Foundation");
        object random=R(randomType),stream=randomType.GetMethod("Get").Invoke(random,new object[]{"medical:surgery-outcomes"});
        var streamType=T("DungeonStory.Foundation.IRandomStream","DungeonStory.Foundation");
        result.Log("WIM023 CONTROLLED_INITIAL_SURGERY_RNG before={0};new=1;onlyBeforeFirstSchedule=true",streamType.GetProperty("State").GetValue(stream));
        streamType.GetMethod("Restore").Invoke(stream,new object[]{1UL});
        Click(root.Find("SurgeryPanel/Commands/Schedule").GetComponent<UnityEngine.UI.Button>());
        result.Log("WIM023 ACTUAL_SCHEDULE_UI details={0}",P(root.Find("SurgeryPanel/Details").GetComponent(tmp),"text"));
        var active=Rows(surgeryType.GetProperty("ActiveOrders").GetValue(surgery));
        if(active.Length!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual UI did not schedule exactly one order.");
        object order=active[0];
        if((string)P(order,"procedureId")!="procedure:prosthetic-installation"||(string)P(order,"targetNodeId")!="arm:left"
          ||(string)P(P(order,"subject"),"subjectId")!="character:staff:1:01"||(string)P(order,"preferredDoctorId")!="owner")
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual UI scheduled wrong subject/doctor/procedure.");
        string selected=(string)P(order,"selectedPartInstanceId");
        var part=prepared.Single(p=>(string)P(p,"partInstanceId")==selected);
        var stackType=T("IWorldItemStackRuntime");var stacks=R(stackType);
        var spawn=stackType.GetMethods().Single(m=>m.Name=="SpawnItemAt"&&m.GetParameters().Length==6);
        object loose=Enum.Parse(spawn.GetParameters()[3].ParameterType,"Loose");
        foreach(var req in Rows(P(order,"materials")).Where(r=>!(bool)P(r,"optional")))
        {
            int count=(int)P(req,"quantity");object[] args={P(req,"itemId"),count,new Vector2Int(43,1),loose,string.Empty,0};
            if(!(bool)spawn.Invoke(stacks,args)||(int)args[5]!=count)throw new InvalidOperationException("Exact ordinary-source preparation failed:"+P(req,"itemId"));
            result.Log("WIM023 ORDINARY_SOURCE item={0};quantity={1};destination=none;position=43,1;haul=normalAI",P(req,"itemId"),count);
        }
        object[] waterArgs={"resource:clean-water",1,new Vector2Int(43,1),loose,string.Empty,0};
        if(!(bool)spawn.Invoke(stacks,waterArgs)||(int)waterArgs[5]!=1)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Authored M03 manual process-water source unavailable.");
        result.Log("WIM023 FIRST_ORDER_SCHEDULED id={0};part={1};instance={2};quality={3};state={4};work={5}/{6};materialsDestination={7};sourceOnlyProcessWater=1;surgeryWork=actualAIOnly",
            P(order,"orderId"),selected,P(part,"physicalItemInstanceId"),P(part,"quality"),P(order,"state"),P(order,"completedWork"),P(order,"requiredWork"),P(order,"materialDestinationId"));
        result.Log("WIM023 FIRST_ORDER_READY game={0};real={1};stillPaused=true",Time.time,EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup);
    }
}
```
