```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        var type=value.GetType(); var prop=type.GetProperty(name);
        if(prop!=null)return prop.GetValue(value);
        var field=type.GetField(name);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(value);
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing public member:"+type.FullName+"."+name);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }


    static object[] Rows(object value) => ((IEnumerable)value).Cast<object>().ToArray();
    static string Id(object part) => (string)P(part,"partInstanceId");
    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying||!EditorApplication.isPaused||Time.timeScale!=0f)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Protected paused medical setup required.");
        var facility=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("BuildableObject")).OfType<Component>()
            .Single(f=>f.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&Convert.ToString(P(P(f,"PersistentInstanceId"),"Value"))=="building:3614a5dde488443ebca1d006e3090f99");
        var worker=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("CharacterActor")).OfType<Component>()
            .Single(a=>a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded&&(string)P(P(a,"Identity"),"PersistentId")=="owner");
        object recipe=Resources.LoadAll("SO/Economy/Recipes",T("ProductionRecipeSO"))
            .Single(r=>(string)P(r,"RecipeId")=="recipe:surgery:prosthetic-arm");
        var queryType=T("IProductionBillQuery");var query=R(queryType);
        var ordersType=T("IProductionBillOrderCommand");var orders=R(ordersType);
        var workType=T("IProductionBillWorkExecution");var work=R(workType);
        var partsType=T("ISurgicalPartRuntime");var parts=R(partsType);
        var stackType=T("IWorldItemStackRuntime");var stacks=R(stackType);
        var getBills=queryType.GetMethod("GetBills");
        if(Rows(getBills.Invoke(query,new object[]{facility})).Any(b=>(string)P(b,"RecipeId")=="recipe:surgery:prosthetic-arm"))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Preparation bill already exists; inspect instead of replay.");
        var capturedBefore=Rows(partsType.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null));
        if(capturedBefore.Any(p=>(string)P(p,"itemDefinitionId")=="surgery:prosthetic:arm:left"
            && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace((string)P(p,"sourceProductionCommitId"))))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Production-backed preparation part already exists; inspect instead of replay.");
        var before=new HashSet<string>(capturedBefore.Select(Id),StringComparer.Ordinal);
        object added=ordersType.GetMethod("AddBill").Invoke(orders,new object[]{facility,"recipe:surgery:prosthetic-arm",
            Enum.Parse(T("ProductionOrderMode","DungeonStory.Production"),"RepeatCount"),2});
        if(!(bool)P(added,"Succeeded"))throw new InvalidOperationException("M06 AddBill rejected:"+P(added,"Failure"));
        object billId=P(added,"BillId");
        result.Log("WIM023 PREP_BILL id={0};cycles=2;gameplayProductionProof=false",P(billId,"Value"));
        var spawn=stackType.GetMethods().Single(m=>m.Name=="SpawnItemAt"&&m.GetParameters().Length==6);
        object bufferState=Enum.Parse(spawn.GetParameters()[3].ParameterType,"FacilityBuffer");
        for(int cycle=0;cycle<2;cycle++)
        {
            object bill=Rows(getBills.Invoke(query,new object[]{facility})).Single(b=>P(b,"BillId").Equals(billId));
            foreach(object input in Rows(P(bill,"Inputs")))
            {
                int count=(int)P(input,"Amount");
                object[] args={P(input,"ItemId"),count,P(facility,"centerPos"),bufferState,P(bill,"MaterialDestinationId"),0};
                if(!(bool)spawn.Invoke(stacks,args)||(int)args[5]!=count)
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("Exact M06 fixture input rejected:"+P(input,"ItemId"));
                result.Log("WIM023 PREP_INPUT cycle={0};item={1};quantity={2};destination={3}",cycle,P(input,"ItemId"),count,P(bill,"MaterialDestinationId"));
            }
            object available=workType.GetMethod("CheckWorkAvailability").Invoke(work,new object[]{facility,P(recipe,"WorkTypeId")});
            if(!(bool)P(available,"Available"))throw new InvalidOperationException("M06 unavailable:"+P(available,"Failure"));
            object begun=workType.GetMethod("BeginWork").Invoke(work,new object[]{worker,facility,P(recipe,"WorkTypeId")});
            if(!(bool)P(begun,"Succeeded"))throw new InvalidOperationException("M06 BeginWork rejected:"+P(begun,"Failure"));
            object completed=workType.GetMethod("ExecuteWork").Invoke(work,new object[]{worker,facility,billId,(float)P(recipe,"RequiredWork")+1f});
            result.Log("WIM023 PREP_CYCLE cycle={0};succeeded={1};completed={2};outcome={3};failure={4}",cycle,P(completed,"Succeeded"),P(completed,"CycleCompleted"),P(completed,"Outcome"),P(completed,"Failure"));
            if(!(bool)P(completed,"Succeeded")||!(bool)P(completed,"CycleCompleted"))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("M06 cycle incomplete; inspect saved WIP, do not replay.");
        }
        var made=Rows(partsType.GetMethod("CaptureParts").Invoke(parts,null)).Where(p=>!before.Contains(Id(p))).ToArray();
        var physical=Rows(stackType.GetMethod("GetAllStacks").Invoke(stacks,null));
        if(made.Length!=2 || made.Select(Id).Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count()!=2
            ||made.Select(p=>(string)P(p,"physicalItemInstanceId")).Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count()!=2
            ||made.Select(p=>(string)P(p,"sourceProductionCommitId")).Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count()!=2)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Two separate production-backed part identities required.");
        foreach(var p in made)
        {
            string item=(string)P(p,"itemDefinitionId"),instance=(string)P(p,"physicalItemInstanceId"),stackId=(string)P(p,"worldStackId");
            if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Id(p))||string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(instance)||string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace((string)P(p,"sourceProductionCommitId"))
              ||item!="surgery:prosthetic:arm:left" ||(string)P(p,"nodeId")!="arm:left" ||(bool)P(p,"installed"))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Invalid prepared part:"+Id(p));
            var matches=physical.Where(s=>(string)P(s,"StackId")==stackId&&(string)P(s,"ItemInstanceId")==instance
                &&(string)P(s,"ItemId")==item&&(int)P(s,"Quantity")==1).ToArray();
            if(matches.Length!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Prepared part physical join invalid:"+Id(p));
            result.Log("WIM023 PREP_PART id={0};instance={1};stack={2};commit={3};quality={4};state={5}",Id(p),instance,stackId,P(p,"sourceProductionCommitId"),P(p,"quality"),P(matches[0],"State"));
        }
        result.Log("WIM023 TWO_REAL_PARTS_READY exactOutputCount=2;directProductionWork=fixtureOnly;surgeryApplyWorkCalls=0;actualSurgery=NOT_RUN");
    }
}
```

### Declared bounded fixture preparation (root-owned, EXECUTED_PASS; setup only)

Sol source audit: existing SurgeryPlayModeVerifier uses public ICharacterProficiencyCommand.AddDirectExperience (Medicine900, Scholarship300) then actual authored qualification. Existing BlueprintResearchRuntime.TryCompleteProjectImmediatelyForVerification publishes real authored unlock state. This command applies only those setup seams, traverses authored research prerequisites, and does not count them as research/training gameplay evidence. Legal facility placement, exact physical production and final real UI/haul/doctor-AI/replacement remain separate gates. No fake medical authority or direct surgery work is used.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp") =>
        Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    static object P(object value, string name) => value.GetType().GetProperty(name).GetValue(value);
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope"))
            .OfType<Component>().Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }
    static void Visit(object project, HashSet<string> visiting, HashSet<string> visited, List<object> order)
    {
        string id = Convert.ToString(P(project, "ProjectId"));
        if (visited.Contains(id)) return;
        if (!visiting.Add(id)) throw new InvalidOperationException("Authored research cycle: " + id);
        foreach (object prerequisite in ((IEnumerable)P(project, "Prerequisites")).Cast<object>()
            .OrderBy(v => Convert.ToString(P(v, "ProjectId")), StringComparer.Ordinal))
            Visit(prerequisite, visiting, visited, order);
        visiting.Remove(id); visited.Add(id); order.Add(project);
    }
    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if (!EditorApplication.isPlaying || Time.timeScale != 0f)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Requires protected, frozen-time Orc party.");
        var actorType = T("CharacterActor");
        object doctor = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(actorType).OfType<Component>()
            .Single(a => a.gameObject.scene.isLoaded && a.gameObject.activeInHierarchy
                && (string)P(P(a, "Identity"), "PersistentId") == "owner");
        if (Convert.ToBoolean(P(doctor, "IsDead")) || Convert.ToString(P(doctor, "characterType")) != "NPC"
            || (string)P(doctor, "SpeciesTag") != "Orc")
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Expected actual living Orc NPC owner.");
        object performance = R(T("ICharacterPerformanceQuery"));
        object procedure = Resources.LoadAll("SO/Medical/Procedures", T("SurgicalProcedureSO", "DungeonStory.Medical"))
            .Single(p => (string)P(p, "ProcedureId") == "procedure:prosthetic-installation");
        var qualified = T("ProcedureOperatorRequirementRuntimeExtensions").GetMethod("IsQualified");
        object[] scoreArgs = { P(procedure, "OperatorRequirement"), doctor, P(procedure, "Family"), performance, 0f, null };
        bool wasQualified = (bool)qualified.Invoke(null, scoreArgs);
        float beforeScore = Convert.ToSingle(scoreArgs[4]);
        var commandType = T("ICharacterProficiencyCommand", "DungeonStory.Characters");
        object proficiency = R(commandType);
        var addExperience = commandType.GetMethod("AddDirectExperience");
        object calendar = R(T("IGameCalendar", "DungeonStory.CoreSession"));
        object typedDoctorId = P(P(doctor, "Identity"), "TypedPersistentId");
        var builtins = T("BuiltInCharacterProficiencyIds", "DungeonStory.Foundation");
        object medicine = builtins.GetField("Medicine").GetValue(null);
        object scholarship = builtins.GetField("Scholarship").GetValue(null);
        // Prepare authored research closure before making any fixture mutation.
        var projectType = T("ResearchProjectSO", "DungeonStory.Research");
        object target = Resources.LoadAll("SO/Research/Projects", projectType)
            .Single(p => Convert.ToString(P(p, "ProjectId")) == "research:medical:prosthetics");
        var projectOrder = new List<object>();
        Visit(target, new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal),
            new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal), projectOrder);
        object research = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(T("BlueprintResearchRuntime")).OfType<Component>()
            .Single(c => c.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object state = P(research, "State");
        object projects = P(state, "Projects");
        var completed = projects.GetType().GetMethod("IsCompleted");
        var complete = research.GetType().GetMethod("TryCompleteProjectImmediatelyForVerification");
        if (completed == null || complete == null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Declared research verification APIs unavailable.");
        long medicineAward = 0, scholarshipAward = 0;
        if (!wasQualified)
        {
            long hour = Convert.ToInt64(P(calendar, "AbsoluteHour"));
            medicineAward = Convert.ToInt64(addExperience.Invoke(proficiency,
                new object[] { typedDoctorId, medicine, 900f, hour, false }));
            scholarshipAward = Convert.ToInt64(addExperience.Invoke(proficiency,
                new object[] { typedDoctorId, scholarship, 300f, hour, false }));
        }
        bool accepts = (bool)qualified.Invoke(null, scoreArgs);
        result.Log("WIM023 FIXTURE_DOCTOR id=owner;beforeScore={0};afterScore={1};qualified={2};medicineAward={3};scholarshipAward={4};setupOnly=true;qualificationGameplayProof=false",
            beforeScore, scoreArgs[4], accepts, medicineAward, scholarshipAward);
        if (!accepts)
        {
            result.Log("WIM023 FIXTURE_NOT_READY reason=DECLARED_DOCTOR_PREP_BELOW_AUTHORED_THRESHOLD;researchUnchanged=true;actualSurgery=NOT_RUN");
            return;
        }
        foreach (object project in projectOrder)
        {
            object id = P(project, "ProjectId");
            if ((bool)completed.Invoke(projects, new[] { id })) continue;
            object[] args = { id, null };
            if (!(bool)complete.Invoke(research, args))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Research fixture failed: " + id + ": " + args[1]);
            if (!(bool)completed.Invoke(projects, new[] { id }))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Research completion not published: " + id);
            result.Log("WIM023 FIXTURE_RESEARCH id={0};completed=true;setupOnly=true", id);
        }
        foreach (int id in new[] { 9503, 9504, 9505, 9506 })
            if (!(bool)state.GetType().GetMethod("IsBuildingUnlocked").Invoke(state, new object[] { id }))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Expected authored medical building unlock: " + id);
        result.Log("WIM023 FIXTURE_DOCTOR_RESEARCH_READY doctor=owner;researchClosureCount={0};buildingUnlocks=9503,9504,9505,9506;actualSurgery=NOT_RUN;facilitiesAndPhysicalParts=NOT_READY;setupOnly=true",
            projectOrder.Count);
    }
}
```

## Root-owned readiness command (2026-09-10, executed)

Use the existing protected wim-048-protected-setup-runcommand.txt Stage A/B verbatim except OwnerOption_1001 -> OwnerOption_1002 and log prefix WIM048 -> WIM023. Dispose automatic save/meta services before clicks; preserve original user files. Then run this read-only qualification query. A READY result proves preparation only, never surgery, research, facility or material completion.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;

internal class CommandScript : IRunCommand
{
    static Type T(string name, string assembly = "Assembly-CSharp")
    {
        return Type.GetType(name + ", " + assembly, true);
    }
    static object P(object value, string name)
    {
        return value.GetType().GetProperty(name)
            .GetValue(value);
    }
    static object R(Type type)
    {
        Type scopeType = T("DungeonRuntimeLifetimeScope");
        Component scope = Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll(scopeType).OfType<Component>()
            .Single(v => v.gameObject.scene.isLoaded);
        object container = P(scope, "Container");
        return container.GetType().GetMethod("Resolve", new[] { typeof(Type), typeof(object) })
            .Invoke(container, new object[] { type, null });
    }
    public void Execute(ExecutionResult result)
    {
        if (!EditorApplication.isPlaying || Time.timeScale != 0f)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Requires same protected, frozen-time Orc preparation.");
        Type worldType = T("ICharacterWorldQuery");
        object world = R(worldType);
        object[] actors = ((IEnumerable)worldType.GetProperty("Characters").GetValue(world))
            .Cast<object>().Where(a => a is Component c && c.gameObject.scene.isLoaded
                && c.gameObject.activeInHierarchy).ToArray();
        if (actors.Length != 3)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Expected three actual committed actors.");
        var ids = actors.Select(a => (string)P(P(a, "Identity"), "PersistentId")).ToArray();
        if (ids.Any(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace) || ids.Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count() != 3)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual actor IDs are missing or duplicated.");
        if (actors.Any(a => !string.Equals((string)P(a, "SpeciesTag"), "Orc",
            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Normal owner selection did not produce three Orc actors.");
        Type captivityType = T("ICaptivityRuntime");
        object captivity = R(captivityType);
        var isCaptive = captivityType.GetMethod("IsCaptive", new[] { typeof(string) });
        object[] npcs = actors.Where(a => !Convert.ToBoolean(P(a, "IsDead"))
            && Convert.ToString(P(a, "characterType")) == "NPC"
            && !(bool)isCaptive.Invoke(captivity, new object[] {
                (string)P(P(a, "Identity"), "PersistentId") })).ToArray();
        if (npcs.Length != 3)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Expected owner and two living noncaptive NPC staff.");
        object procedure = Resources.LoadAll("SO/Medical/Procedures",
            T("SurgicalProcedureSO", "DungeonStory.Medical"))
            .Single(p => (string)P(p, "ProcedureId") == "procedure:prosthetic-installation");
        Type profilesType = T("IAnatomyProfileCatalog", "DungeonStory.Medical");
        object profiles = R(profilesType);
        object performance = R(T("ICharacterPerformanceQuery"));
        Type anatomyType = T("IAnatomyHealthRuntime");
        object anatomy = R(anatomyType);
        var qualified = T("ProcedureOperatorRequirementRuntimeExtensions")
            .GetMethod("IsQualified");
        string doctorId = null;
        foreach (object actor in npcs)
        {
            string id = (string)P(P(actor, "Identity"), "PersistentId");
            object profile = profilesType.GetMethod("GetForSpecies")
                .Invoke(profiles, new object[] { (string)P(actor, "SpeciesTag") });
            object[] nodeArgs = { "arm:left", null };
            bool hasArm = (bool)profile.GetType().GetMethod("TryGetNode").Invoke(profile, nodeArgs);
            bool removable = hasArm && Convert.ToBoolean(P(nodeArgs[1], "Removable"));
            if ((string)P(profile, "ProfileId") != "anatomy:orc"
                || (string)P(profile, "AnatomyFamily") != "humanoid" || !removable)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Staff lacks authored Orc removable arm profile: " + id);
            object liveBody = anatomyType.GetMethod("GetAnatomySnapshot", new[] { T("CharacterActor") })
                .Invoke(anatomy, new[] { actor });
            object liveArm = ((IEnumerable)P(liveBody, "Nodes")).Cast<object>()
                .SingleOrDefault(n => (string)n.GetType().GetField("nodeId").GetValue(n) == "arm:left");
            if (liveArm == null || (bool)liveArm.GetType().GetField("missing").GetValue(liveArm)
                || Convert.ToSingle(liveArm.GetType().GetField("currentHealth").GetValue(liveArm)) <= 0f)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Fresh actual staff arm state is unavailable: " + id);
            object[] qualificationArgs = {
                P(procedure, "OperatorRequirement"), actor, P(procedure, "Family"),
                performance, 0f, null };
            bool accepts = (bool)qualified.Invoke(null, qualificationArgs);
            if (accepts && doctorId == null) doctorId = id;
            result.Log("WIM023 STAFF id={0};profile={1};arm=arm:left;liveArmHealthy=true;qualified={2};score={3};failure={4}",
                id, P(profile, "ProfileId"), accepts, qualificationArgs[4], qualificationArgs[5]);
        }
        if (doctorId == null)
        {
            result.Log("WIM023 PREPARATION_NOT_READY reason=NO_QUALIFIED_NPC_DOCTOR;actualActors=3;actualOrcStaff=2;noSkillsChanged=true;actualSurgery=NOT_RUN");
            return;
        }
        string patientId = npcs.Select(a => (string)P(P(a, "Identity"), "PersistentId"))
            .First(id => id != doctorId && id != "owner");
        result.Log("WIM023 PREPARATION_READY patient={0};doctor={1};actualActors=3;actualOrcStaff=2;arm=arm:left;procedure=procedure:prosthetic-installation;normalUI=true;ownerUsesActualNpcEligibility=true;noSkillsChanged=true;researchFacilityMaterials=NOT_READY;actualSurgery=NOT_RUN",
            patientId, doctorId);
    }
}
```
